                        sourceLabel: 'Zincir (' + monthNamesShort[chain.month] + ' ' + chain.year + ') ' + (adjustPct >= 0 ? '+' : '') + adjustPct + '%',
                        confidence: 'low', sourceColor: 'orange',
                        needsAdjustment: true, adjustKey, adjustPct
                    };
                }
                return {
                    avgNightly: 0, totalRevenue: 0, totalNights: 0, count: 0,
                    source: 'veri-yok', sourceLabel: 'Veri Yok', confidence: 'none', sourceColor: 'red'
                };
            }
        },

        updateChainAdjust(key, value) {
            this.chainAdjustments[key] = parseFloat(value) || 0;
            this.calculate();
            this.save();
        },

        calculate() {
            const today = new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
            const defaultAdjust = parseFloat(document.getElementById('projDefaultAdjust')?.value) || 0;

            // Daire verisi yoksa çık — son 3 takvim ayında veri girilmemiş daireler inaktif sayılır
            const _projNow = new Date();
            const _activeMonthKeys = new Set();
            for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                const d = new Date(_projNow.getFullYear(), _projNow.getMonth() - i, 1);
                _activeMonthKeys.add(d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0'));
            }
            const _monthKey = ds => { if (!ds) return ''; const d = new Date(ds); return d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0'); };
            const allApartments = Analytics.data?.apartments || [];
            const apartments = allApartments.filter(apt => {
                const hasRecentRes = (Analytics.data?.reservations || []).some(r =>
                    r.apartmentId === apt.id && _activeMonthKeys.has(_monthKey(r.checkIn))
                );
                const hasRecentExp = (Analytics.data?.expenses || []).some(e =>
                    e.apartmentId === apt.id && _activeMonthKeys.has(_monthKey(e.date))
                );
                return hasRecentRes || hasRecentExp;
            });
            if (apartments.length === 0) {
                document.getElementById('projSummaryCards')?.classList.add('hidden');
                document.getElementById('projMonthlySection')?.classList.add('hidden');
                document.getElementById('projResTableSection')?.classList.add('hidden');
                return;
            }

            // --- 1. 12 aylık periyot oluştur ---
            const now = new Date();
            const startMonth = now.getMonth();
            const startYear = now.getFullYear();
            const months12 = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
                let m = startMonth + i, y = startYear;
                if (m >= 12) { m -= 12; y++; }
                const monthKey = y + '-' + String(m + 1).padStart(2, '0');
                months12.push({ month: m, year: y, monthKey });
            }

            // --- 2. Geçmiş verilerden oranlar (platform komisyon, banka/nakit) ---
            const hist = this.getHistoricalRatios();

            // KDV sistemleri
            const kdvSystems = new Set(['1','3','4','5','9']);
            const kdvBookingOnlySystems = new Set(['7']);

            let totalEstGross = 0, totalEstExpenses = 0, totalEstKDV = 0, totalEstPlatComm = 0, totalLagirioNet = 0, totalOwnerNet = 0;
            const monthlyData = {};
            const aptTotals = {};
            const aptMonthlyDetail = {};
            let totalResCount = 0;

            // --- 3. Her daire × 12 ay karma tahmin ---
            apartments.forEach(apartment => {
                const aptId = apartment.id;
                const sysType = apartment.systemType || '?';
                const taxRate = apartment.taxRate || 0;
                const stdExpense = this.getStandardExpense(aptId);

                // Platform komisyon ve banka/nakit oranları
                const platCommRatio = hist.platformCommRate[aptId] ?? hist.avgPlatformCommRate;
                const bankCashRatio = hist.bankRatio[aptId] ?? hist.avgBankRatio;

                if (!aptTotals[aptId]) aptTotals[aptId] = { id: aptId, code: apartment.code || aptId, systemType: sysType, commission: apartment.lagirioCommission || 0, gross: 0, expenses: 0, kdv: 0, platComm: 0, lagirio: 0, ownerShare: 0, months: 0, avgNightly: 0, count: 0, totalNights: 0, nightlyProfit: 0 };
                if (!aptMonthlyDetail[aptId]) aptMonthlyDetail[aptId] = {};

                months12.forEach(({ month, year, monthKey }) => {
                    // --- Karma gelir tahmini ---
                    const estimate = this.estimateMonthRevenue(aptId, month, year, defaultAdjust);
                    const avgNightly = estimate.avgNightly;
                    // Brüt gelir = ort. gecelik fiyat × doluluk günü (tam ay tahmini)
                    const estMonthlyGross = avgNightly * this.OCCUPANCY_DAYS;

                    // --- Gider: daire bazlı standart (ekstrem eliminasyonlu) ---
                    const estExpenses = stdExpense;

                    // --- Platform komisyonu ---
                    const estPlatformComm = estMonthlyGross * platCommRatio;

                    // --- KDV ---
                    let estKDV = 0;
                    const bankAmount = estMonthlyGross * bankCashRatio;
                    if (sysType === '2') {
                        // Sistem 2: iade edilen tutarın %20 KDV'si
                        const bankPlatComm2 = bankAmount * platCommRatio;
                        const lagirioComm2 = bankAmount * ((apartment.lagirioCommission || 0) / 100);
                        const toBeReturned2 = estExpenses + bankPlatComm2 + lagirioComm2;
                        estKDV = toBeReturned2 * 0.20;
                    } else if (taxRate > 0) {
                        if (kdvSystems.has(sysType)) {
                            estKDV = bankAmount * (taxRate / 100) / (1 + taxRate / 100);
                        } else if (kdvBookingOnlySystems.has(sysType)) {
                            const bookingPortion = bankAmount * 0.7;
                            estKDV = bookingPortion * (taxRate / 100) / (1 + taxRate / 100);
                        }
                    }

                    // --- Lagirio kazancı ---
                    let lagirioEarning = 0;
                    let ownerEarning = 0;
                    const cashAmount = estMonthlyGross * (1 - bankCashRatio);
                    const fakeReservations = [];
                    if (bankAmount > 0) {
                        fakeReservations.push({ totalAmount: bankAmount, currency: 'EUR', platformCommission: bankAmount * platCommRatio, paymentMethod: 'Banka', platform: 'Booking.com' });
                    }
                    if (cashAmount > 0) {
                        fakeReservations.push({ totalAmount: cashAmount, currency: 'EUR', platformCommission: cashAmount * platCommRatio, paymentMethod: 'Nakit', platform: 'Direkt' });
                    }
                    const fakeExpenses = [{ amount: estExpenses, currency: 'EUR' }];

                    if (Analytics.calculateLagirioRevenue) {
                        lagirioEarning = Analytics.calculateLagirioRevenue(apartment, fakeReservations, fakeExpenses);
                    } else {
                        lagirioEarning = (estMonthlyGross - estPlatformComm - estExpenses) * 0.15;
                    }

                    if (Analytics.calculateOwnerShare) {
                        ownerEarning = Analytics.calculateOwnerShare(apartment, fakeReservations, fakeExpenses);
                    } else {
                        ownerEarning = estMonthlyGross - estPlatformComm - estExpenses - lagirioEarning;
                    }

                    // --- Topla ---
                    totalEstGross += estMonthlyGross;
                    totalEstExpenses += estExpenses;
                    totalEstKDV += estKDV;
                    totalEstPlatComm += estPlatformComm;
                    totalLagirioNet += lagirioEarning;
                    totalOwnerNet += ownerEarning;
                    totalResCount += estimate.count;

                    // Aylık veri
                    if (!monthlyData[monthKey]) monthlyData[monthKey] = { gross: 0, expenses: 0, kdv: 0, platComm: 0, lagirio: 0, ownerShare: 0, count: 0, avgNightly: 0, aptCount: 0 };
                    monthlyData[monthKey].gross += estMonthlyGross;
                    monthlyData[monthKey].expenses += estExpenses;
                    monthlyData[monthKey].kdv += estKDV;
                    monthlyData[monthKey].platComm += estPlatformComm;
                    monthlyData[monthKey].lagirio += lagirioEarning;
                    monthlyData[monthKey].ownerShare += ownerEarning;
                    monthlyData[monthKey].count += estimate.count;
                    monthlyData[monthKey].avgNightly += avgNightly;
                    monthlyData[monthKey].aptCount++;

                    // Daire toplamı
                    aptTotals[aptId].gross += estMonthlyGross;
                    aptTotals[aptId].expenses += estExpenses;
                    aptTotals[aptId].kdv += estKDV;
                    aptTotals[aptId].platComm += estPlatformComm;
                    aptTotals[aptId].lagirio += lagirioEarning;
                    aptTotals[aptId].ownerShare += ownerEarning;
                    aptTotals[aptId].months++;
                    aptTotals[aptId].avgNightly += avgNightly;
                    aptTotals[aptId].count += estimate.count;
                    aptTotals[aptId].totalNights += (estimate.totalNights || this.OCCUPANCY_DAYS);

                    // Daire-ay detayı
                    aptMonthlyDetail[aptId][monthKey] = {
                        gross: estMonthlyGross,
                        actualRevenue: estimate.totalRevenue,
                        expenses: estExpenses,
                        kdv: estKDV,
                        platComm: estPlatformComm,
                        lagirio: lagirioEarning,
                        ownerShare: ownerEarning,
                        avgNightly: avgNightly,
                        resCount: estimate.count,
                        totalNights: estimate.totalNights,
                        source: estimate.source,
                        sourceLabel: estimate.sourceLabel,
                        sourceColor: estimate.sourceColor,
                        confidence: estimate.confidence,
                        needsAdjustment: estimate.needsAdjustment,
                        adjustKey: estimate.adjustKey,
                        adjustPct: estimate.adjustPct
                    };
                });

                // Daire ortalamasını düzelt
                if (aptTotals[aptId].months > 0) aptTotals[aptId].avgNightly = aptTotals[aptId].avgNightly / aptTotals[aptId].months;
                // Gecelik kâr hesapla
                if (aptTotals[aptId].totalNights > 0) {
                    aptTotals[aptId].nightlyProfit = aptTotals[aptId].lagirio / aptTotals[aptId].totalNights;
                }
            });

            // Ay bazlı ortalamaları düzelt
            Object.values(monthlyData).forEach(m => {
                if (m.aptCount > 0) m.avgNightly = m.avgNightly / m.aptCount;
            });

            // --- Şirket gideri tahmini ve gerçek kâr ---
            const avgMonthlyLagirio = totalLagirioNet / 12;
            const estMonthlyCompanyExp = this.estimateMonthlyCompanyExpense(avgMonthlyLagirio);
            const totalCompanyExpense = estMonthlyCompanyExp * 12;
            const realProfit = totalLagirioNet - totalCompanyExpense;

            // monthlyData'ya companyExpense ve realProfit ekle
            Object.keys(monthlyData).forEach(mk => {
                monthlyData[mk].companyExpense = estMonthlyCompanyExp;
                monthlyData[mk].realProfit = monthlyData[mk].lagirio - estMonthlyCompanyExp;
            });

            // Cache for trend/comparison panels
            this._lastMonthlyData = monthlyData;
            this._lastAptMonthlyDetail = aptMonthlyDetail;
            this._lastAptTotals = aptTotals;
            this._estMonthlyCompanyExp = estMonthlyCompanyExp;
            this._totalCompanyExpense = totalCompanyExpense;
            this._realProfit = realProfit;

            // --- 4. UI Güncelle ---
            // Önce container'ları görünür yap (ECharts boyut hesabı için şart)
            document.getElementById('projSummaryCards')?.classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('projMonthlySection')?.classList.remove('hidden');

            this.renderSummaryCards(totalEstGross, totalEstExpenses, totalEstKDV, totalEstPlatComm, totalLagirioNet, totalOwnerNet, totalResCount, Object.keys(aptTotals).length, totalCompanyExpense, realProfit, estMonthlyCompanyExp);
            this.renderMonthlyChart(monthlyData);
            this.renderMonthlyOverview(monthlyData, aptMonthlyDetail);
            this.renderApartmentList(Object.values(aptTotals), aptMonthlyDetail);
            this.renderTable();
            const futureRes = this.reservations.filter(r => new Date(r.checkIn) >= today);
            this.renderPlatformFilter(futureRes);
            // Owner heading güncellemeleri
            const isOwner = Auth.currentUser?.role === 'owner';
            const chartTitle = document.getElementById('projChartTitle');
            const tableTitle = document.getElementById('projTableTitle');
            const tableSubtitle = document.getElementById('projTableSubtitle');
            if (chartTitle) {
                chartTitle.textContent = isOwner ? t('proj.owner.monthly.title', 'Aylık Projeksiyon (Net Kazancınız)') : t('proj.monthly.title', 'Aylık Projeksiyon (Lagirio Kazancı)');
                if (isOwner) chartTitle.removeAttribute('data-i18n');
            }
            if (tableTitle) {
                tableTitle.textContent = isOwner ? t('proj.owner.monthly.detail', 'Aylık Kazanç Detayı') : t('proj.monthly.detail', 'Aylık Lagirio Kazanç Detayı');
                if (isOwner) tableTitle.removeAttribute('data-i18n');
            }
            if (tableSubtitle) tableSubtitle.textContent = isOwner ? t('proj.owner.all.total', '(tüm daireleriniz toplam)') : t('proj.all.apts.total', '(tüm daireler toplam)');
            if (!isOwner) {
                document.getElementById('projResTableSection')?.classList.remove('hidden');
                // Admin kârlılık paneli (Ay-Ay + Aralık)
                document.getElementById('projAdminProfitPanel')?.classList.remove('hidden');
                this.switchProfitView(this._profitViewMode || 'monthly');
            } else {
                document.getElementById('projResTableSection')?.classList.add('hidden');
                document.getElementById('projAdminProfitPanel')?.classList.add('hidden');
            }
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        },

        // Geçmiş verilerden daire bazlı oranları çıkar
        getHistoricalRatios() {
            const result = {
                expenseRatio: {},      // aptId → gider/gelir oranı
                platformCommRate: {},   // aptId → platform komisyon/gelir oranı
                bankRatio: {},          // aptId → banka gelir / toplam gelir oranı
                avgExpenseRatio: 0.12,
                avgPlatformCommRate: 0.15,
                avgBankRatio: 0.85      // varsayılan %85 banka
            };

            if (!Analytics.data?.apartments) return result;

            let gTotalGross = 0, gTotalExp = 0, gTotalPlatComm = 0, gTotalBank = 0;

            Analytics.data.apartments.forEach(apt => {
                const reses = Analytics.data.reservations?.filter(r => r.apartmentId === apt.id) || [];
                const exps = Analytics.data.expenses?.filter(e => e.apartmentId === apt.id) || [];

                const aptGross = reses.reduce((s, r) => s + (Analytics.convertToEUR ? Analytics.convertToEUR(r.totalAmount, r.currency || 'EUR') : (r.totalAmount || 0)), 0);
                const aptExp = exps.reduce((s, e) => s + (Analytics.convertToEUR ? Analytics.convertToEUR(e.amount, e.currency) : (e.amount || 0)), 0);
                const aptPlatComm = reses.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.platformCommission || 0), 0);
                const aptBankIncome = reses.filter(r => r.paymentMethod !== 'Nakit')
                    .reduce((s, r) => s + (Analytics.convertToEUR ? Analytics.convertToEUR(r.totalAmount, r.currency || 'EUR') : (r.totalAmount || 0)), 0);

                if (aptGross > 0) {
                    result.expenseRatio[apt.id] = aptExp / aptGross;
                    result.platformCommRate[apt.id] = aptPlatComm / aptGross;
                    result.bankRatio[apt.id] = aptBankIncome / aptGross;
                }

                gTotalGross += aptGross;
                gTotalExp += aptExp;
                gTotalPlatComm += aptPlatComm;
                gTotalBank += aptBankIncome;
            });

            if (gTotalGross > 0) {
                result.avgExpenseRatio = gTotalExp / gTotalGross;
                result.avgPlatformCommRate = gTotalPlatComm / gTotalGross;
                result.avgBankRatio = gTotalBank / gTotalGross;
            }

            // Mapping: PMS aptId → Analytics aptId ise, oranları PMS ID'ye de kopyala
            const mapping = this.loadMapping();
            Object.entries(mapping).forEach(([pmsId, analyticsId]) => {
                if (pmsId !== analyticsId) {
                    if (result.expenseRatio[analyticsId] !== undefined) result.expenseRatio[pmsId] = result.expenseRatio[analyticsId];
                    if (result.platformCommRate[analyticsId] !== undefined) result.platformCommRate[pmsId] = result.platformCommRate[analyticsId];
                    if (result.bankRatio[analyticsId] !== undefined) result.bankRatio[pmsId] = result.bankRatio[analyticsId];
                }
            });

            return result;
        },

        // ---- Render Functions ----
        renderSummaryCards(estGross, estExpenses, estKDV, estPlatComm, lagirioNet, ownerNet, resCount, aptCount, companyExpense, realProfit, monthlyCompExp) {
            const isOwner = Auth.currentUser?.role === 'owner';
            companyExpense = companyExpense || 0;
            realProfit = realProfit || 0;
            monthlyCompExp = monthlyCompExp || 0;

            // \u00dcst sat\u0131r: Br\u00fct, Ana Kazan\u00e7, Gecelik
            document.getElementById('projGrossRevenue').textContent = '\u20ac' + estGross.toFixed(2);
            document.getElementById('projGrossDetail').textContent = aptCount + ' ' + t('common.apartment', 'daire') + ' | 12 ' + t('proj.col.month', 'ay').toLowerCase() + ' | ' + t('proj.blend', 'Karma tahmin');

            if (isOwner) {
                const mainLabel = document.getElementById('projMainLabel');
                if (mainLabel) { mainLabel.textContent = t('proj.est.net.income', 'Tahmini Net Kazancınız'); mainLabel.removeAttribute('data-i18n'); }
                document.getElementById('projLagirioNet').textContent = '\u20ac' + ownerNet.toFixed(2);
                const ownerRatio = estGross > 0 ? Math.round((ownerNet / estGross) * 100) : 0;
                document.getElementById('projLagirioDetail').textContent = t('proj.gross.pct', 'Brüt gelirin %') + ownerRatio + (I18n.currentLang === 'tr' ? '\'i' : '%');
            } else {
                document.getElementById('projMainLabel').textContent = t('proj.est.gross.short', 'Brüt Kazanç (Dairelerden)');
                document.getElementById('projLagirioNet').textContent = '\u20ac' + lagirioNet.toFixed(2);
                const lagirioRatio = estGross > 0 ? Math.round((lagirioNet / estGross) * 100) : 0;
                document.getElementById('projLagirioDetail').textContent = t('proj.gross.pct', 'Brüt gelirin %') + lagirioRatio + (I18n.currentLang === 'tr' ? '\'i' : '%');

                // Ger\u00e7ek K\u00e2r kart\u0131
                const rpEl = document.getElementById('projRealProfit');
                if (rpEl) {
                    rpEl.textContent = '\u20ac' + realProfit.toFixed(2);
                    rpEl.className = realProfit >= 0 ? 'text-2xl font-bold' : 'text-2xl font-bold text-red-200';
                }
                const ceEl = document.getElementById('projCompanyExpense');
                if (ceEl) ceEl.textContent = '\u20ac' + companyExpense.toFixed(2);
                const noteEl = document.getElementById('projCompanyExpNote');
                if (noteEl) noteEl.textContent = '(\u20ac' + monthlyCompExp.toFixed(0) + '/ay tahmin)';
                const marginEl = document.getElementById('projProfitMargin');
                if (marginEl) {
                    const margin = lagirioNet > 0 ? ((realProfit / lagirioNet) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';
                    marginEl.textContent = '%' + margin;
                }
                // Grid 3\u21924 s\u00fctun
                const topGrid = document.getElementById('projTopGrid');
                if (topGrid) {
                    topGrid.classList.remove('md:grid-cols-3');
                    topGrid.classList.add('md:grid-cols-4');
                }
                document.getElementById('projRealProfitCard')?.classList.remove('hidden');
            }

            const avgNightly = estGross > 0 ? estGross / (aptCount * 12 * this.OCCUPANCY_DAYS || 1) : 0;
            document.getElementById('projAvgNightly').textContent = '\u20ac' + avgNightly.toFixed(2);
            document.getElementById('projNightlyDetail').textContent = aptCount + ' ' + t('common.apartment', 'daire') + ' | ' + this.OCCUPANCY_DAYS + ' ' + t('proj.days.month', 'gün/ay') + ' | ' + t('proj.gross', 'Brüt') + '/' + (aptCount*12) + ' ' + t('proj.col.month', 'ay');

            // Alt satır: Gider, KDV, Platform Komis. (+ Owner: Lagirio Komis.)
            document.getElementById('projExpenses').textContent = '\u20ac' + estExpenses.toFixed(2);
            document.getElementById('projExpenseDetail').textContent = t('proj.expense.detail', 'Daire bazlı standart (ekstrem eleme)');

            document.getElementById('projKDV').textContent = '\u20ac' + estKDV.toFixed(2);
            const kdvRatio = estGross > 0 ? ((estKDV / estGross) * 100).toFixed(1) : 0;
            document.getElementById('projKDVDetail').textContent = t('proj.of.gross', 'Brütün %') + kdvRatio + (I18n.currentLang === 'tr' ? '\'i' : '%');

            document.getElementById('projPlatComm').textContent = '\u20ac' + estPlatComm.toFixed(2);
            const platRatio = estGross > 0 ? Math.round((estPlatComm / estGross) * 100) : 0;
            document.getElementById('projPlatCommDetail').textContent = t('proj.hist.ratio', 'Geçmiş oranı: %') + platRatio;

            // Owner: 4. kart - Lagirio Komisyonu göster + grid 4 sütun yap
            const lagirioCommCard = document.getElementById('projLagirioCommCard');
            const deductionGrid = document.getElementById('projDeductionGrid');
            if (isOwner) {
                if (lagirioCommCard) {
                    lagirioCommCard.classList.remove('hidden');
                    document.getElementById('projLagirioComm').textContent = '\u20ac' + lagirioNet.toFixed(2);
                    const lagirioCommRatio = estGross > 0 ? Math.round((lagirioNet / estGross) * 100) : 0;
                    document.getElementById('projLagirioCommDetail').textContent = t('proj.of.gross', 'Brütün %') + lagirioCommRatio + (I18n.currentLang === 'tr' ? '\'i' : '%');
                }
                if (deductionGrid) deductionGrid.classList.replace('md:grid-cols-3', 'md:grid-cols-4');
            } else {
                if (lagirioCommCard) lagirioCommCard.classList.add('hidden');
                if (deductionGrid) {
                    deductionGrid.classList.remove('md:grid-cols-4');
                    deductionGrid.classList.add('md:grid-cols-3');
                }
            }

            // Owner: Waterfall diyagramı render et
            if (isOwner) {
                this.renderOwnerWaterfall(estGross, estPlatComm, lagirioNet, estKDV, estExpenses, ownerNet);
            } else {
                const wf = document.getElementById('projOwnerWaterfall');
                if (wf) wf.classList.add('hidden');
            }
        },

        renderOwnerWaterfall(estGross, estPlatComm, lagirioComm, estKDV, estExpenses, ownerNet) {
            const wf = document.getElementById('projOwnerWaterfall');
            if (!wf) return;
            wf.classList.remove('hidden');

            const steps = [
                { label: t('proj.gross.income', 'Brüt Gelir'), value: estGross, color: 'bg-blue-500', textColor: 'text-blue-700' },
                { label: t('proj.platform.comm.short', 'Platform Kom.'), value: -estPlatComm, color: 'bg-orange-400', textColor: 'text-orange-600' },
                { label: t('proj.lagirio.comm.short', 'Lagirio Kom.'), value: -lagirioComm, color: 'bg-indigo-400', textColor: 'text-indigo-600' },
                { label: t('proj.kdv.taxes', 'KDV/Vergiler'), value: -estKDV, color: 'bg-red-400', textColor: 'text-red-600' },
                { label: t('proj.expenses', 'Giderler'), value: -estExpenses, color: 'bg-amber-400', textColor: 'text-amber-600' },
                { label: t('proj.your.net', 'Net Kazancınız'), value: ownerNet, color: 'bg-emerald-500', textColor: 'text-emerald-700', isResult: true }
            ];

            const maxVal = estGross;
            const barWidth = maxVal > 0 ? 100 : 0;

            let running = estGross;
            let html = '<div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-lg p-6">' +
                '<h3 class="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-800 mb-4 flex items-center space-x-2">' +
                '<i data-lucide="git-branch" class="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600"></i>' +
                '<span>' + t('proj.waterfall.title', 'Kazanç Akışı') + '</span>' +
                '<span class="text-xs text-gray-400 ml-2">(' + t('proj.waterfall.desc', 'Brüt gelirden net kazanca') + ')</span></h3>';

            html += '<div class="space-y-2">';

            steps.forEach((step, i) => {
                if (i === 0) {
                    // Br\u00fct Gelir - full bar
                    const w = maxVal > 0 ? (step.value / maxVal * 100) : 0;
                    html += '<div class="flex items-center gap-3">' +
                        '<div class="w-28 text-right text-xs font-medium text-gray-600">' + step.label + '</div>' +
                        '<div class="flex-1 h-8 bg-gray-100 rounded-lg relative overflow-hidden">' +
                        '<div class="h-full ' + step.color + ' rounded-lg flex items-center justify-end pr-2 transition-all" style="width:' + w.toFixed(1) + '%">' +
                        '<span class="text-white text-xs font-bold">\u20ac' + step.value.toFixed(0) + '</span></div></div>' +
                        '<div class="w-20 text-right text-xs font-bold ' + step.textColor + '">\u20ac' + step.value.toFixed(0) + '</div></div>';
                } else if (step.isResult) {
                    // Net sonu\u00e7 - ye\u015fil bar
                    const w = maxVal > 0 ? (step.value / maxVal * 100) : 0;
                    html += '<div class="border-t-2 border-dashed border-gray-300 pt-2 mt-1"></div>' +
                        '<div class="flex items-center gap-3">' +
                        '<div class="w-28 text-right text-xs font-bold text-emerald-700">' + step.label + '</div>' +
                        '<div class="flex-1 h-10 bg-gray-100 rounded-lg relative overflow-hidden">' +
                        '<div class="h-full ' + step.color + ' rounded-lg flex items-center justify-end pr-2 transition-all" style="width:' + w.toFixed(1) + '%">' +
                        '<span class="text-white text-sm font-bold">\u20ac' + step.value.toFixed(0) + '</span></div></div>' +
                        '<div class="w-20 text-right text-sm font-bold ' + step.textColor + '">\u20ac' + step.value.toFixed(0) + '</div></div>';
                } else {
                    // Kesinti - ince bar (sa\u011fdan sola do\u011fru)
                    const absVal = Math.abs(step.value);
                    const w = maxVal > 0 ? (absVal / maxVal * 100) : 0;
                    running += step.value; // running decreases
                    const offset = maxVal > 0 ? (running / maxVal * 100) : 0;
                    html += '<div class="flex items-center gap-3">' +
                        '<div class="w-28 text-right text-xs font-medium text-gray-500">' + step.label + '</div>' +
                        '<div class="flex-1 h-6 bg-gray-100 rounded-lg relative overflow-hidden">' +
                        '<div class="absolute h-full ' + step.color + ' rounded-sm opacity-80" style="left:' + offset.toFixed(1) + '%;width:' + w.toFixed(1) + '%"></div>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '<div class="w-20 text-right text-xs font-medium ' + step.textColor + '">-\u20ac' + absVal.toFixed(0) + '</div></div>';
                }
            });

            // Percentage summary
            const pctOwner = estGross > 0 ? (ownerNet / estGross * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';
            const pctDeductions = estGross > 0 ? ((estGross - ownerNet) / estGross * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';
            html += '</div>' +
                '<div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-6 mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-gray-100 text-xs text-gray-500">' +
                '<span>' + t('proj.gross.income', 'Brüt Gelir') + ': <strong class="text-gray-700">\u20ac' + estGross.toFixed(0) + '</strong></span>' +
                '<span class="text-gray-300">|</span>' +
                '<span>' + t('proj.total.deduction', 'Toplam Kesinti') + ': <strong class="text-red-500">%' + pctDeductions + '</strong></span>' +
                '<span class="text-gray-300">|</span>' +
                '<span>' + t('proj.your.share', 'Elinize Geçen') + ': <strong class="text-emerald-600">%' + pctOwner + '</strong></span>' +
                '</div></div>';

            wf.innerHTML = html;
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        },

        // --- Admin: K\u00e2rl\u0131l\u0131k Analizi View Modlar\u0131 ---
        switchProfitView(mode) {
            this._profitViewMode = mode;
            ['monthly','range'].forEach(m => {
                const btn = document.getElementById('profitView' + m.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + m.slice(1));
                if (btn) {
                    btn.className = m === mode
                        ? 'px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium rounded-md bg-white shadow text-gray-800'
                        : 'px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium rounded-md text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800';
                }
            });
            document.getElementById('profitRangeSelector')?.classList.toggle('hidden', mode !== 'range');

            if (mode === 'range') this.populateRangeSelectors();
            this.renderProfitView();
        },

        renderProfitView() {
            const mode = this._profitViewMode || 'monthly';
            if (mode === 'monthly') this.renderProfitViewMonthly();
            else if (mode === 'range') this.renderProfitViewRange();
        },

        renderProfitViewMonthly() {
            const el = document.getElementById('profitViewContent');
            if (!el || !this._lastMonthlyData) return;
            const monthlyData = this._lastMonthlyData;
            const aptDetail = this._lastAptMonthlyDetail || {};
            const compExp = this._estMonthlyCompanyExp || 0;
            const monthNames = I18n.getMonthNames('short');
            const sortedKeys = Object.keys(monthlyData).sort();

            let grandLagirio = 0, grandCompExp = 0, grandReal = 0, grandGross = 0, grandExp = 0, grandKdv = 0, grandPlat = 0;
            sortedKeys.forEach(k => {
                const d = monthlyData[k];
                grandLagirio += d.lagirio; grandCompExp += compExp; grandReal += d.realProfit;
                grandGross += d.gross; grandExp += d.expenses; grandKdv += d.kdv; grandPlat += d.platComm;
            });

            let html = '<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs border-collapse"><thead><tr class="bg-gray-50 text-gray-500">';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-left font-medium">' + t('proj.col.month', 'Ay') + '</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.col.apt', 'Daire') + '</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.col.res', 'Rez.') + '</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.col.gross', 'Brüt Gelir') + '</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.col.apt.expense', 'Daire Gider') + '</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.col.vat', 'KDV') + '</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.col.plt.comm', 'Plt.Kom') + '</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium text-blue-600">' + t('proj.col.gross.profit', 'Brüt Kazanç') + '</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium text-amber-600">' + t('proj.col.company.exp', 'Şirket Gideri') + '</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium text-indigo-600">' + t('proj.col.real.profit', 'Gerçek Kâr') + '</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.col.margin', 'Marj') + '</th>';
            html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';

            sortedKeys.forEach((mk, idx) => {
                const d = monthlyData[mk];
                const [y, m] = mk.split('-');
                const mName = monthNames[parseInt(m)-1] + ' ' + y;
                const realP = d.lagirio - compExp;
                const margin = d.lagirio > 0 ? ((realP / d.lagirio) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';
                const realClass = realP >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-red-600';

                html += '<tr class="border-t border-gray-100 hover:bg-indigo-50/40 cursor-pointer transition-colors" onclick="Projection.toggleProfitMonthDetail(' + idx + ')">';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 font-medium text-gray-700"><i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-3 h-3 inline mr-1 text-gray-400 transition-transform" id="profitMonthChev_' + idx + '"></i>' + mName + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-gray-600">' + d.aptCount + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-gray-600">' + d.count + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-gray-700">\u20ac' + d.gross.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-orange-500">\u20ac' + d.expenses.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-red-500">\u20ac' + d.kdv.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-indigo-400">\u20ac' + d.platComm.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-bold text-blue-600">\u20ac' + d.lagirio.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-amber-600">\u20ac' + compExp.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-bold ' + realClass + '">\u20ac' + realP.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-gray-500">%' + margin + '</td>';
                html += '</tr>';

                // Expandable apt detail
                html += '<tr id="profitMonthRow_' + idx + '" class="hidden"><td colspan="11" class="p-0">';
                html += '<div class="bg-indigo-50/30 px-4 py-3 border-t border-indigo-100">';
                const aptRows = [];
                Object.entries(aptDetail).forEach(([aptId, months]) => {
                    const md = months[mk];
                    if (md && md.lagirio > 0) {
                        const apt = (Analytics.data?.apartments || []).find(a => a.id === aptId);
                        const code = apt?.code || aptId;
                        const nights = md.totalNights || Projection.OCCUPANCY_DAYS;
                        aptRows.push({ code, lagirio: md.lagirio, gross: md.gross, nights, nightlyProfit: md.lagirio / (nights || 1), resCount: md.resCount || 0, profitPct: md.gross > 0 ? (md.lagirio / md.gross * 100) : 0 });
                    }
                });
                aptRows.sort((a, b) => b.nightlyProfit - a.nightlyProfit);

                if (aptRows.length > 0) {
                    html += '<table class="w-full text-[11px]"><thead><tr class="text-gray-400">';
                    html += '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-left">' + t('proj.col.apt', 'Daire') + '</th>';
                    html += '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right">' + t('proj.per.night.profit', 'Kâr/Gece') + '</th>';
                    html += '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right">' + t('proj.total.profit', 'Toplam Kâr') + '</th>';
                    html += '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right">' + t('proj.gross', 'Brüt') + '</th>';
                    html += '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right">' + t('proj.night', 'Gece') + '</th>';
                    html += '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right">' + t('proj.col.res', 'Rez.') + '</th>';
                    html += '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right">' + t('proj.profitability', 'Kârlılık') + '</th>';
                    html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';
                    aptRows.forEach(a => {
                        html += '<tr class="border-t border-gray-100/60">';
                        html += '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 font-medium text-gray-700">' + a.code + '</td>';
                        html += '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right font-bold text-emerald-600">\u20ac' + a.nightlyProfit.toFixed(2) + '</td>';
                        html += '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right text-gray-700">\u20ac' + a.lagirio.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
                        html += '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right text-gray-500">\u20ac' + a.gross.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
                        html += '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right text-gray-500">' + a.nights + '</td>';
                        html += '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right text-gray-500">' + a.resCount + '</td>';
                        html += '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right text-indigo-600">%' + a.profitPct.toFixed(1) + '</td>';
                        html += '</tr>';
                    });
                    html += '</tbody></table>';
                } else {
                    html += '<p class="text-xs text-gray-400 text-center py-2">' + t('proj.no.detail', 'Bu ayda daire verisi yok') + '</p>';
                }
                html += '</div></td></tr>';
            });

            // Toplam sat\u0131r\u0131
            const totalMargin = grandLagirio > 0 ? ((grandReal / grandLagirio) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';
            const totalRealClass = grandReal >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-red-600';
            html += '<tr class="border-t-2 border-gray-300 font-bold bg-gray-50">';
            html += '<td class="py-2.5 px-3" colspan="3">' + t('proj.total.12m', 'TOPLAM (12 Ay)') + '</td>';
            html += '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right">\u20ac' + grandGross.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
            html += '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-orange-500">\u20ac' + grandExp.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
            html += '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-red-500">\u20ac' + grandKdv.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
            html += '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-indigo-400">\u20ac' + grandPlat.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
            html += '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-blue-600">\u20ac' + grandLagirio.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
            html += '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-amber-600">\u20ac' + grandCompExp.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
            html += '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right ' + totalRealClass + '">\u20ac' + grandReal.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
            html += '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right">%' + totalMargin + '</td>';
            html += '</tr></tbody></table></div>';

            el.innerHTML = html;
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        },

        toggleProfitMonthDetail(idx) {
            const row = document.getElementById('profitMonthRow_' + idx);
            const chev = document.getElementById('profitMonthChev_' + idx);
            if (!row) return;
            const isHidden = row.classList.contains('hidden');
            row.classList.toggle('hidden');
            if (chev) chev.style.transform = isHidden ? 'rotate(90deg)' : '';
        },

        populateRangeSelectors() {
            const startSel = document.getElementById('profitRangeStart');
            const endSel = document.getElementById('profitRangeEnd');
            if (!startSel || !endSel || !this._lastMonthlyData) return;
            const monthNames = I18n.getMonthNames('short');
            const keys = Object.keys(this._lastMonthlyData).sort();
            const opts = keys.map(k => { const [y,m] = k.split('-'); return '<option value="' + k + '">' + monthNames[parseInt(m)-1] + ' ' + y + '</option>'; }).join('');
            if (!startSel.options.length) { startSel.innerHTML = opts; endSel.innerHTML = opts; endSel.selectedIndex = keys.length - 1; }
        },

        applyProfitRange() {
            this.renderProfitViewRange();
        },

        renderProfitViewRange() {
            const el = document.getElementById('profitViewContent');
            if (!el || !this._lastMonthlyData) return;
            const monthlyData = this._lastMonthlyData;
            const aptDetail = this._lastAptMonthlyDetail || {};
            const aptTotals = this._lastAptTotals || {};
            const compExp = this._estMonthlyCompanyExp || 0;
            const monthNames = I18n.getMonthNames('short');

            const startKey = document.getElementById('profitRangeStart')?.value;
            const endKey = document.getElementById('profitRangeEnd')?.value;
            const allKeys = Object.keys(monthlyData).sort();
            const rangeKeys = allKeys.filter(k => (!startKey || k >= startKey) && (!endKey || k <= endKey));

            if (rangeKeys.length === 0) { el.innerHTML = '<p class="text-sm text-gray-400 text-center py-4">' + t('proj.no.data', 'Seçilen aralıkta veri yok') + '</p>'; return; }

            // Toplam
            let totLagirio = 0, totCompExp = 0, totGross = 0;
            rangeKeys.forEach(k => { totLagirio += monthlyData[k].lagirio; totCompExp += compExp; totGross += monthlyData[k].gross; });
            const totReal = totLagirio - totCompExp;
            const margin = totLagirio > 0 ? ((totReal / totLagirio) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';

            const rangeLabel = (function() {
                const [sy, sm] = rangeKeys[0].split('-');
                const [ey, em] = rangeKeys[rangeKeys.length-1].split('-');
                return monthNames[parseInt(sm)-1] + ' ' + sy + ' \u2013 ' + monthNames[parseInt(em)-1] + ' ' + ey;
            })();

            let html = '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3 mb-4">';
            html += '<div class="bg-blue-50 rounded-lg p-3 text-center"><p class="text-[10px] text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.gross.profit', 'Brüt Kazanç') + '</p><p class="text-lg font-bold text-blue-600">\u20ac' + totLagirio.toFixed(0) + '</p></div>';
            html += '<div class="bg-amber-50 rounded-lg p-3 text-center"><p class="text-[10px] text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.company.exp', 'Şirket Gideri') + '</p><p class="text-lg font-bold text-amber-600">\u20ac' + totCompExp.toFixed(0) + '</p></div>';
            html += '<div class="bg-' + (totReal >= 0 ? 'emerald' : 'red') + '-50 rounded-lg p-3 text-center"><p class="text-[10px] text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.real.profit', 'Gerçek Kâr') + '</p><p class="text-lg font-bold ' + (totReal >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-red-600') + '">\u20ac' + totReal.toFixed(0) + '</p></div>';
            html += '<div class="bg-indigo-50 rounded-lg p-3 text-center"><p class="text-[10px] text-gray-500">' + t('proj.profit.margin', 'Kâr Marjı') + '</p><p class="text-lg font-bold text-indigo-600">%' + margin + '</p></div>';
            html += '</div>';
            html += '<p class="text-xs text-gray-400 mb-3">' + rangeLabel + ' | ' + rangeKeys.length + ' ' + t('proj.col.month', 'ay') + '</p>';

            // Daire baz\u0131nda k\u00e2rl\u0131l\u0131k s\u0131ralamas\u0131
            const aptData = [];
            Object.entries(aptDetail).forEach(([aptId, months]) => {
                let aptLagirio = 0, aptGross = 0, aptNights = 0, aptRes = 0;
                rangeKeys.forEach(mk => {
                    const md = months[mk];
                    if (md) { aptLagirio += md.lagirio; aptGross += md.gross; aptNights += (md.totalNights || Projection.OCCUPANCY_DAYS); aptRes += (md.resCount || 0); }
                });
                if (aptLagirio > 0 || aptGross > 0) {
                    const apt = (Analytics.data?.apartments || []).find(a => a.id === aptId);
                    aptData.push({ code: apt?.code || aptId, lagirio: aptLagirio, gross: aptGross, nights: aptNights, res: aptRes, nightlyProfit: aptNights > 0 ? aptLagirio / aptNights : 0, profitPct: aptGross > 0 ? (aptLagirio / aptGross * 100) : 0 });
                }
            });
            aptData.sort((a, b) => b.nightlyProfit - a.nightlyProfit);

            html += '<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs"><thead class="bg-gray-50"><tr>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-left font-medium text-gray-500">#</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-left font-medium text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.apt', 'Daire') + '</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium text-gray-500">' + t('proj.avg.nightly.profit', 'Ort.Gecelik Kâr') + '</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium text-gray-500">' + t('proj.total.profit', 'Toplam Kâr') + '</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium text-gray-500">' + t('proj.gross', 'Brüt') + '</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium text-gray-500">' + t('proj.night', 'Gece') + '</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.res', 'Rez.') + '</th>';
            html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium text-gray-500">' + t('proj.profitability', 'Kârlılık') + '</th>';
            html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';

            aptData.forEach((a, i) => {
                html += '<tr class="border-t border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 font-bold text-gray-300">' + (i+1) + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 font-medium text-gray-700">' + a.code + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-bold text-emerald-600">\u20ac' + a.nightlyProfit.toFixed(2) + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-gray-700">\u20ac' + a.lagirio.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-gray-500">\u20ac' + a.gross.toFixed(0) + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-gray-500">' + a.nights + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-gray-500">' + a.res + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-indigo-600 font-medium">%' + a.profitPct.toFixed(1) + '</td>';
                html += '</tr>';
            });
            html += '</tbody></table></div>';

            el.innerHTML = html;
        },


        getMonthData(monthKey) {
            // Projeksiyondan veya ge\u00e7mi\u015f Analytics'ten ay verisi
            if (this._lastMonthlyData && this._lastMonthlyData[monthKey]) return this._lastMonthlyData[monthKey];
            // Ge\u00e7mi\u015f y\u0131l i\u00e7in Analytics'ten hesapla
            const [y, m] = monthKey.split('-');
            const apartments = Analytics.data?.apartments || [];
            let gross = 0, lagirio = 0, expenses = 0, kdv = 0, platComm = 0, count = 0, aptCount = 0;
            apartments.forEach(apt => {
                const monthRes = (Analytics.data?.reservations || []).filter(r => {
                    const rd = new Date(r.checkIn);
                    return r.apartmentId === apt.id && rd.getMonth() === parseInt(m)-1 && rd.getFullYear() === parseInt(y);
                });
                const monthExp = (Analytics.data?.expenses || []).filter(e => {
                    const ed = new Date(e.date);
                    return e.apartmentId === apt.id && ed.getMonth() === parseInt(m)-1 && ed.getFullYear() === parseInt(y);
                });
                if (monthRes.length > 0) {
                    const convertEUR = Analytics.convertToEUR ? Analytics.convertToEUR.bind(Analytics) : (a) => a;
                    const g = monthRes.reduce((s, r) => s + convertEUR(r.totalAmount || r.totalPrice || 0, r.currency || 'EUR'), 0);
                    const l = Analytics.calculateLagirioRevenue ? Analytics.calculateLagirioRevenue(apt, monthRes, monthExp) : 0;
                    const e = monthExp.reduce((s, ex) => s + convertEUR(ex.amount, ex.currency), 0);
                    gross += g; lagirio += l; expenses += e; count += monthRes.length; aptCount++;
                }
            });
            if (gross === 0 && lagirio === 0) return null;
            const compExp = this._estMonthlyCompanyExp || 0;
            return { gross, lagirio, expenses, kdv: 0, platComm: 0, count, aptCount, companyExpense: compExp, realProfit: lagirio - compExp };
        },

        // Eski projeksiyon trend grafikleri kaldırıldı — yeni versiyonlar Analytics.createProfitTrendCharts() içinde

        // --- Admin: Şirket gideri tahmini (orantılı ayarlama ile) ---
        estimateMonthlyCompanyExpense(projectedMonthlyProfit) {
            const exps = Analytics.data?.lagirioExpenses || [];
            if (exps.length === 0) return 0;
            const convertEUR = Analytics.convertToEUR ? Analytics.convertToEUR.bind(Analytics) : (a) => a;

            // Aylık toplama (sadece gider girilen aylar)
            const monthTotals = {};
            exps.forEach(e => {
                const d = new Date(e.date);
                const mk = d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0');
                if (!monthTotals[mk]) monthTotals[mk] = 0;
                monthTotals[mk] += convertEUR(e.amount, e.currency);
            });

            const expMonths = Object.keys(monthTotals);
            if (expMonths.length === 0) return 0;

            const baseMonthlyExp = Object.values(monthTotals).reduce((s,v) => s+v, 0) / expMonths.length;

            // Aynı aylar için geçmiş daire kârı (Lagirio kazancı) hesapla
            let histProfitSum = 0;
            const apartments = Analytics.data?.apartments || [];
            expMonths.forEach(mk => {
                const [y, m] = mk.split('-');
                let monthProfit = 0;
                apartments.forEach(apt => {
                    const monthRes = (Analytics.data?.reservations || []).filter(r => {
                        const rd = new Date(r.checkIn);
                        return r.apartmentId === apt.id && rd.getMonth() === parseInt(m)-1 && rd.getFullYear() === parseInt(y);
                    });
                    const monthExp = (Analytics.data?.expenses || []).filter(e => {
                        const ed = new Date(e.date);
                        return e.apartmentId === apt.id && ed.getMonth() === parseInt(m)-1 && ed.getFullYear() === parseInt(y);
                    });
                    if (Analytics.calculateLagirioRevenue) {
                        monthProfit += Analytics.calculateLagirioRevenue(apt, monthRes, monthExp);
                    }
                });
                histProfitSum += monthProfit;
            });
            const baseMonthlyProfit = histProfitSum / expMonths.length;

            // Oran ayarlaması: daire kârı artışına orantılı şirket gideri artırımı
            // Her 1.5x oran artışında %15, doğrusal interpolasyon
            if (baseMonthlyProfit > 0 && baseMonthlyExp > 0 && projectedMonthlyProfit > 0) {
                const histRatio = baseMonthlyProfit / baseMonthlyExp;
                const projRatio = projectedMonthlyProfit / baseMonthlyExp;
                const ratioGrowth = projRatio / histRatio;
                if (ratioGrowth > 1) {
                    const multiplier = 1 + (ratioGrowth - 1) * 0.3;
                    return baseMonthlyExp * multiplier;
                }
            }

            return baseMonthlyExp;
        },

        renderMonthlyChart(monthlyData) {
            const container = document.getElementById('projMonthlyChart');
            if (!container) return;
            const isOwner = Auth.currentUser?.role === 'owner';
            const monthNames = I18n.getMonthNames('short');
            const sortedKeys = Object.keys(monthlyData).sort();

            if (sortedKeys.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<p class="text-gray-400 text-sm text-center py-8">' + t('proj.no.data.label', 'Veri yok') + '</p>';
                return;
            }

            const maxVal = Math.max(...sortedKeys.map(k => monthlyData[k].gross));

            if (isOwner) {
                // Owner: ECharts — Net Kazanç (bar) + Brüt Gelir (line), tıklanabilir
                container.innerHTML = '';
                if (!this._charts) this._charts = {};
                if (this._charts._projOwnerMonthly) {
                    try { this._charts._projOwnerMonthly.dispose(); } catch(e) {}
                }
                const chart = echarts.init(container);
                this._charts._projOwnerMonthly = chart;

                const labels = sortedKeys.map(k => { const [y, m] = k.split('-'); return monthNames[parseInt(m)-1] + ' ' + y.slice(2); });
                const netData = sortedKeys.map(k => parseFloat((monthlyData[k].ownerShare || 0).toFixed(2)));
                const grossData = sortedKeys.map(k => parseFloat(monthlyData[k].gross.toFixed(2)));
                const avgNightlyData = sortedKeys.map(k => parseFloat(monthlyData[k].avgNightly.toFixed(2)));
                const countData = sortedKeys.map(k => monthlyData[k].count);

                chart.setOption({
                    tooltip: {
                        trigger: 'axis', backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.85)', borderColor: 'transparent', textStyle: { fontSize: 12 },
                        formatter: function(params) {
                            const idx = params[0].dataIndex;
                            let html = '<div style="font-weight:600;margin-bottom:6px">' + params[0].axisValue + '</div>';
                            params.forEach(function(p) { html += '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;margin:3px 0"><span style="display:inline-block;width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:' + p.color + '"></span>' + p.seriesName + ': <strong>€' + p.value.toLocaleString() + '</strong></div>'; });
                            html += '<div style="margin-top:6px;padding-top:6px;border-top:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.15);font-size:11px;color:#aaa">' + countData[idx] + ' ' + t('proj.res.short', 'rez') + ' · €' + avgNightlyData[idx].toFixed(2) + '/' + t('proj.night', 'gece') + '</div>';
                            html += '<div style="font-size:10px;color:#6ee7b7;margin-top:2px">' + t('proj.click.detail', 'Detay için tıklayın') + '</div>';
                            return html;
                        }
                    },
                    legend: { data: [t('proj.your.net', 'Net Kazancınız'), t('proj.gross.income', 'Brüt Gelir')], bottom: 0, textStyle: { fontSize: 11 } },
                    grid: { left: '2%', right: '3%', bottom: '15%', top: '8%', containLabel: true },
                    xAxis: { type: 'category', data: labels, axisLabel: { rotate: 45, fontSize: 10 }, axisTick: { alignWithLabel: true } },
                    yAxis: { type: 'value', axisLabel: { formatter: function(v) { return '€' + (v >= 1000 ? (v/1000).toFixed(0) + 'k' : v); }, fontSize: 10 }, splitLine: { lineStyle: { color: '#f3f4f6', type: 'dashed' } } },
                    series: [
                        {
                            name: t('proj.your.net', 'Net Kazancınız'), type: 'bar', data: netData, barWidth: '45%',
                            itemStyle: { color: new echarts.graphic.LinearGradient(0,0,0,1,[{offset:0,color:'#10b981'},{offset:1,color:'#059669'}]), borderRadius: [6,6,0,0] },
                            label: { show: true, position: 'top', formatter: function(p) { return '€' + p.value.toLocaleString(); }, fontSize: 9, color: '#059669', fontWeight: 'bold' }
                        },
                        {
                            name: t('proj.gross.income', 'Brüt Gelir'), type: 'line', data: grossData, smooth: true,
                            itemStyle: { color: '#3b82f6' }, lineStyle: { width: 2.5 }, symbol: 'circle', symbolSize: 7,
                            areaStyle: { color: new echarts.graphic.LinearGradient(0,0,0,1,[{offset:0,color:'rgba(59,130,246,0.12)'},{offset:1,color:'rgba(59,130,246,0.02)'}]) }
                        }
                    ]
                });

                // Container boyut güncelleme (hidden → visible geçişinde gerekebilir)
                setTimeout(function() { chart.resize(); }, 100);

                // Tıklama: ay detay modalını aç
                const self = this;
                chart.on('click', function(params) {
                    if (params.dataIndex !== undefined) {
                        const mk = sortedKeys[params.dataIndex];
                        self.showOwnerMonthDetail(mk);
                    }
                });
            } else {
                // Admin: mevcut g\u00f6r\u00fcn\u00fcm + Ger\u00e7ek K\u00e2r notu
                const compExp = this._estMonthlyCompanyExp || 0;
                container.innerHTML = '<div class="flex items-end gap-3 h-56">' + sortedKeys.map(key => {
                    const d = monthlyData[key];
                    const [y, m] = key.split('-');
                    const label = monthNames[parseInt(m)-1] + ' ' + y.slice(2);
                    const grossH = maxVal > 0 ? Math.max(8, (d.gross / maxVal) * 190) : 8;
                    const lagirioH = maxVal > 0 ? Math.max(4, (d.lagirio / maxVal) * 190) : 4;
                    const expH = maxVal > 0 ? Math.max(2, (d.expenses / maxVal) * 190) : 2;
                    const realP = d.lagirio - compExp;
                    const realClass = realP >= 0 ? 'text-indigo-600' : 'text-red-500';

                    return '<div class="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center gap-1">' +
                        '<div class="text-center mb-1">' +
                        '<span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-600 block">\u20ac' + d.lagirio.toFixed(0) + '</span>' +
                        '<span class="text-[9px] ' + realClass + ' font-bold">\u20ac' + realP.toFixed(0) + ' net</span></div>' +
                        '<div class="w-full flex gap-0.5 items-end justify-center" style="height:' + grossH + 'px">' +
                        '<div class="flex-1 bg-emerald-100 rounded-t-sm h-full relative">' +
                        '<div class="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 bg-emerald-500 rounded-t-sm" style="height:' + lagirioH + 'px"></div></div>' +
                        '<div class="w-2 bg-orange-300 rounded-t-sm" style="height:' + expH + 'px"></div></div>' +
                        '<span class="text-[10px] text-gray-600 whitespace-nowrap font-medium">' + label + '</span>' +
                        '<span class="text-[9px] text-gray-400">' + d.count + ' ' + t('common.res.short', 'rez.') + ' | ' + d.aptCount + ' ' + t('common.apartment', 'daire') + '</span>' +
                        '</div>';
                }).join('') + '</div>' +
                '<div class="flex items-center gap-4 mt-3 justify-center text-[10px] text-gray-400">' +
                '<span class="flex items-center gap-1"><span class="w-3 h-3 bg-emerald-100 rounded"></span> ' + t('proj.blend.est.gross', 'Karma Tahmini Brüt') + '</span>' +
                '<span class="flex items-center gap-1"><span class="w-3 h-3 bg-emerald-500 rounded"></span> ' + t('proj.gross.profit', 'Brüt Kazanç') + '</span>' +
                '<span class="flex items-center gap-1"><span class="w-3 h-3 bg-orange-300 rounded"></span> ' + t('proj.std.expense.label', 'Standart Gider') + '</span>' +
                '<span class="flex items-center gap-1"><span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 bg-indigo-500 rounded"></span> ' + t('proj.real.profit.label', 'Gerçek Kâr') + '</span>' +
                '</div>';
            }
        },

        renderMonthlyOverview(monthlyData, aptMonthlyDetail) {
            const container = document.getElementById('projMonthlyOverview');
            if (!container) return;
            const isOwner = Auth.currentUser?.role === 'owner';
            const monthNames = I18n.getMonthNames('full');
            const sortedKeys = Object.keys(monthlyData).sort();

            if (sortedKeys.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<p class="text-gray-400 text-sm text-center py-4">' + t('proj.no.data.label', 'Veri yok') + '</p>';
                return;
            }

            // Grand totals
            let grandGross = 0, grandExp = 0, grandKdv = 0, grandPlat = 0, grandLagirio = 0, grandOwner = 0, grandCount = 0;
            sortedKeys.forEach(k => {
                const d = monthlyData[k];
                grandGross += d.gross; grandExp += d.expenses; grandKdv += d.kdv;
                grandPlat += d.platComm; grandLagirio += d.lagirio; grandOwner += (d.ownerShare || 0); grandCount += d.count;
            });

            if (isOwner) {
                // Owner: 12 aylık kart grid
                this.renderOwnerMonthlyCards(container, monthlyData, aptMonthlyDetail, sortedKeys, monthNames);
                return;
            }

            let html = '<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs border-collapse"><thead><tr class="bg-gray-50 text-gray-500">';

            if (false) {
                // (eski owner tablo kodu — artık kullanılmıyor)
            } else {
                // Admin: t\u00fcm s\u00fctunlar
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-left font-medium">' + t('proj.col.month', 'Ay') + '</th>' +
                    '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.col.res', 'Rez.') + '</th>' +
                    '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.col.apt', 'Daire') + '</th>' +
                    '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.avg.nightly', 'Ort. Gecelik') + '</th>' +
                    '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.col.gross', 'Brüt Gelir') + '</th>' +
                    '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.col.apt.expense', 'Gider') + '</th>' +
                    '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.col.vat', 'KDV') + '</th>' +
                    '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.col.plt.comm', 'Plt.Kom') + '</th>' +
                    '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium text-emerald-700">' + t('proj.col.lagirio', 'Lagirio') + '</th>';
            }
            html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';

            const colSpan = isOwner ? 6 : 9;

            sortedKeys.forEach((mk, idx) => {
                const d = monthlyData[mk];
                const [y, m] = mk.split('-');
                const mName = monthNames[parseInt(m) - 1] + ' ' + y;

                if (isOwner) {
                    const ownerVal = d.ownerShare || 0;
                    const deductions = d.gross - ownerVal;
                    const ownerRatio = d.gross > 0 ? ((ownerVal / d.gross) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';
                    html += '<tr class="border-t border-gray-100 hover:bg-indigo-50/40 cursor-pointer transition-colors" onclick="Projection.toggleMonthlyDetail(' + idx + ')">' +
                        '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-medium text-gray-700"><i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-3 h-3 inline mr-1 text-gray-400 transition-transform" id="projMonthChev_' + idx + '"></i>' + mName + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-gray-600">' + d.count + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-gray-600">\u20ac' + d.avgNightly.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right font-medium text-gray-700">\u20ac' + d.gross.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-red-500">-\u20ac' + deductions.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right font-bold text-emerald-600">\u20ac' + ownerVal.toFixed(2) + ' <span class="text-[9px] text-gray-400 font-normal">%' + ownerRatio + '</span></td>' +
                        '</tr>';
                } else {
                    const lagirioRatio = d.gross > 0 ? ((d.lagirio / d.gross) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';
                    html += '<tr class="border-t border-gray-100 hover:bg-indigo-50/40 cursor-pointer transition-colors" onclick="Projection.toggleMonthlyDetail(' + idx + ')">' +
                        '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 font-medium text-gray-700"><i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-3 h-3 inline mr-1 text-gray-400 transition-transform" id="projMonthChev_' + idx + '"></i>' + mName + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-gray-600">' + d.count + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-gray-600">' + d.aptCount + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-gray-600">\u20ac' + d.avgNightly.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right font-medium text-gray-700">\u20ac' + d.gross.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-orange-500">\u20ac' + d.expenses.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-red-500">\u20ac' + d.kdv.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-indigo-500">\u20ac' + d.platComm.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right font-bold text-emerald-600">\u20ac' + d.lagirio.toFixed(2) + ' <span class="text-[9px] text-gray-400 font-normal">%' + lagirioRatio + '</span></td>' +
                        '</tr>';
                }

                // Expandable apartment detail for this month
                html += '<tr id="projMonthDetail_' + idx + '" class="hidden"><td colspan="' + colSpan + '" class="p-0">';
                html += '<div class="bg-indigo-50/30 px-4 py-3 border-t border-indigo-100">';

                const aptRows = [];
                if (aptMonthlyDetail) {
                    Object.entries(aptMonthlyDetail).forEach(([aptId, months]) => {
                        const md = months[mk];
                        if (md && (md.gross > 0 || md.lagirio > 0 || md.resCount > 0)) {
                            const apartments = (Analytics?.data?.apartments || []);
                            const apt = apartments.find(a => a.id === aptId);
                            const code = apt?.code || aptId;
                            aptRows.push({ code, ...md });
                        }
                    });
                }
                aptRows.sort((a, b) => (isOwner ? (b.ownerShare || 0) - (a.ownerShare || 0) : b.lagirio - a.lagirio));

                if (aptRows.length > 0) {
                    html += '<table class="w-full text-[11px]"><thead><tr class="text-gray-400">';
                    if (isOwner) {
                        html += '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-left font-medium">' + t('proj.th.apt', 'Daire') + '</th>' +
                            '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-left font-medium">' + t('proj.th.source', 'Kaynak') + '</th>' +
                            '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.th.res', 'Rez.') + '</th>' +
                            '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.gross', 'Brüt') + '</th>' +
                            '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.deduction', 'Kesinti') + '</th>' +
                            '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right font-medium text-emerald-700">' + t('proj.net', 'Net') + '</th>';
                    } else {
                        html += '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-left font-medium">' + t('proj.th.apt', 'Daire') + '</th>' +
                            '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-left font-medium">' + t('proj.th.source', 'Kaynak') + '</th>' +
                            '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.th.res', 'Rez.') + '</th>' +
                            '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.th.avg.nightly', 'Ort.Gclk') + '</th>' +
                            '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.gross', 'Brüt') + '</th>' +
                            '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.th.expense', 'Gider') + '</th>' +
                            '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.th.kdv', 'KDV') + '</th>' +
                            '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right font-medium">' + t('proj.th.plat.comm', 'Plt.Kom') + '</th>' +
                            '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right font-medium text-emerald-700">Lagirio</th>';
                    }
                    html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';

                    const sourceColors = { emerald: 'text-emerald-600', amber: 'text-amber-600', blue: 'text-blue-600', orange: 'text-orange-500', red: 'text-red-400' };
                    aptRows.forEach(a => {
                        const sColor = sourceColors[a.sourceColor] || 'text-gray-500';
                        if (isOwner) {
                            const ownerVal = a.ownerShare || 0;
                            const deduction = (a.gross || 0) - ownerVal;
                            html += '<tr class="border-t border-gray-100/60">' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 font-medium text-gray-700">' + a.code + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 ' + sColor + '">' + (a.sourceLabel || '-') + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right text-gray-600">' + (a.resCount || 0) + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right text-gray-700">\u20ac' + (a.gross || 0).toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right text-red-500">-\u20ac' + deduction.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right font-bold text-emerald-600">\u20ac' + ownerVal.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '</tr>';
                        } else {
                            html += '<tr class="border-t border-gray-100/60">' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 font-medium text-gray-700">' + a.code + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 ' + sColor + '">' + (a.sourceLabel || '-') + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right text-gray-600">' + (a.resCount || 0) + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right text-gray-600">\u20ac' + (a.avgNightly || 0).toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right text-gray-700">\u20ac' + (a.gross || 0).toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right text-orange-500">\u20ac' + (a.expenses || 0).toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right text-red-500">\u20ac' + (a.kdv || 0).toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right text-indigo-500">\u20ac' + (a.platComm || 0).toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-right font-bold text-emerald-600">\u20ac' + (a.lagirio || 0).toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '</tr>';
                        }
                    });
                    html += '</tbody></table>';
                } else {
                    html += '<p class="text-gray-400 text-xs py-2">' + t('proj.no.detail', 'Bu ay için daire detayı yok') + '</p>';
                }
                html += '</div></td></tr>';
            });

            // Grand total row
            if (isOwner) {
                const grandOwnerRatio = grandGross > 0 ? ((grandOwner / grandGross) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';
                const grandDeductions = grandGross - grandOwner;
                html += '<tr class="border-t-2 border-gray-300 bg-gray-50 font-bold">' +
                    '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-gray-800">' + t('proj.total.12m', 'TOPLAM (12 Ay)') + '</td>' +
                    '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-gray-700">' + grandCount + '</td>' +
                    '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-gray-500">-</td>' +
                    '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-gray-800">\u20ac' + grandGross.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                    '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-red-600">-\u20ac' + grandDeductions.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                    '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-emerald-700">\u20ac' + grandOwner.toFixed(2) + ' <span class="text-[9px] text-gray-400 font-normal">%' + grandOwnerRatio + '</span></td>' +
                    '</tr>';
            } else {
                const grandRatio = grandGross > 0 ? ((grandLagirio / grandGross) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';
                html += '<tr class="border-t-2 border-gray-300 bg-gray-50 font-bold">' +
                    '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-gray-800">' + t('proj.total.12m', 'TOPLAM (12 Ay)') + '</td>' +
                    '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-gray-700">' + grandCount + '</td>' +
                    '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-gray-500">-</td>' +
                    '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-gray-500">-</td>' +
                    '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-gray-800">\u20ac' + grandGross.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                    '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-orange-600">\u20ac' + grandExp.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                    '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-red-600">\u20ac' + grandKdv.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                    '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-indigo-600">\u20ac' + grandPlat.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                    '<td class="py-2.5 px-3 text-right text-emerald-700">\u20ac' + grandLagirio.toFixed(2) + ' <span class="text-[9px] text-gray-400 font-normal">%' + grandRatio + '</span></td>' +
                    '</tr>';
            }

            html += '</tbody></table></div>';
            container.innerHTML = html;
        },

        toggleMonthlyDetail(idx) {
            const row = document.getElementById('projMonthDetail_' + idx);
            const chev = document.getElementById('projMonthChev_' + idx);
            if (!row) return;
            const isHidden = row.classList.contains('hidden');
            row.classList.toggle('hidden');
            if (chev) chev.style.transform = isHidden ? 'rotate(90deg)' : '';
        },

        // ========= OWNER: 12 Aylık Kart Grid =========
        renderOwnerMonthlyCards(container, monthlyData, aptMonthlyDetail, sortedKeys, monthNames) {
            const maxNet = Math.max(...sortedKeys.map(k => monthlyData[k].ownerShare || 0));

            let html = '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">';

            sortedKeys.forEach(mk => {
                const d = monthlyData[mk];
                const [y, m] = mk.split('-');
                const mName = monthNames[parseInt(m) - 1] + ' ' + y;
                const ownerVal = d.ownerShare || 0;
                const deductions = d.gross - ownerVal;
                const netRatio = maxNet > 0 ? (ownerVal / maxNet) : 0;

                // Renk yoğunluğu: en yüksek kazançlı ay daha belirgin
                const borderClass = netRatio > 0.85 ? 'border-emerald-300 bg-emerald-50/30' : netRatio > 0.5 ? 'border-emerald-200' : 'border-gray-100';
                const barWidth = Math.max(8, netRatio * 100);

                html += '<div class="border ' + borderClass + ' rounded-xl p-3.5 cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md hover:border-emerald-300 transition-all group" onclick="Projection.showOwnerMonthDetail(\'' + mk + '\')">';
                html += '<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">';
                html += '<span class="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">' + mName + '</span>';
                html += '<span class="text-[10px] text-gray-400 group-hover:text-emerald-500 transition-colors"><i data-lucide="arrow-right" class="w-3 h-3 inline"></i></span>';
                html += '</div>';

                // Net kazanç — büyük
                html += '<div class="text-lg font-bold text-emerald-600 mb-1">€' + ownerVal.toFixed(0) + '</div>';

                // Progress bar
                html += '<div class="w-full bg-gray-100 rounded-full h-1.5 mb-2"><div class="bg-emerald-400 h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all" style="width:' + barWidth + '%"></div></div>';

                // Brüt ve kesinti
                html += '<div class="flex justify-between text-[10px] mb-2">';
                html += '<span class="text-gray-500">' + t('proj.gross', 'Brüt') + ' €' + d.gross.toFixed(0) + '</span>';
                html += '<span class="text-red-400">-€' + deductions.toFixed(0) + '</span>';
                html += '</div>';

                // Rez ve gecelik
                html += '<div class="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mb-1.5">' + d.count + ' ' + t('proj.res.short', 'rez') + ' · €' + d.avgNightly.toFixed(0) + '/' + t('proj.night', 'gece') + '</div>';

                html += '</div>';
            });

            html += '</div>';

            // Grand total kartı
            let grandGross = 0, grandOwner = 0, grandCount = 0;
            sortedKeys.forEach(function(k) { grandGross += monthlyData[k].gross; grandOwner += (monthlyData[k].ownerShare || 0); grandCount += monthlyData[k].count; });
            const grandDeductions = grandGross - grandOwner;
            const grandRatio = grandGross > 0 ? ((grandOwner / grandGross) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';

            html += '<div class="mt-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-50 to-emerald-100/50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-xl p-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">';
            html += '<div class="flex items-center gap-3"><i data-lucide="calendar-check-2" class="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600"></i><span class="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700">' + t('proj.12m.total', '12 Aylık Toplam') + '</span></div>';
            html += '<div class="flex items-center gap-6 text-sm">';
            html += '<div><span class="text-gray-500">' + t('proj.gross', 'Brüt') + '</span> <strong class="text-gray-700">€' + grandGross.toFixed(0) + '</strong></div>';
            html += '<div><span class="text-red-400">' + t('proj.deduction', 'Kesinti') + '</span> <strong class="text-red-500">-€' + grandDeductions.toFixed(0) + '</strong></div>';
