            html += '<div><span class="text-emerald-600">' + t('proj.net', 'Net') + '</span> <strong class="text-emerald-700 text-base">€' + grandOwner.toFixed(0) + '</strong> <span class="text-[10px] text-gray-400">%' + grandRatio + '</span></div>';
            html += '<div><span class="text-gray-400">' + grandCount + ' ' + t('proj.res.short', 'rez') + '</span></div>';
            html += '</div></div>';

            container.innerHTML = html;
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        },

        // ========= OWNER: Ay Detay Modal (tabbed) =========
        showOwnerMonthDetail(monthKey) {
            const monthNames = I18n.getMonthNames('full');
            const convertEUR = Analytics.convertToEUR ? Analytics.convertToEUR.bind(Analytics) : function(a) { return a; };
            const fmt = function(d) { const dt = new Date(d); return dt.getDate() + '.' + (dt.getMonth()+1) + '.' + dt.getFullYear(); };
            const [year, month] = monthKey.split('-');
            const y = parseInt(year), m = parseInt(month) - 1;
            const mName = monthNames[m] + ' ' + year;

            // Aylık toplam veriler
            const monthlyData = this._lastMonthlyData || {};
            const md = monthlyData[monthKey] || {};
            const ownerVal = md.ownerShare || 0;
            const deductions = (md.gross || 0) - ownerVal;

            // Daire bazlı detay
            const aptMonthlyDetail = this._aptMonthlyDetail || {};
            const apartments = Analytics?.data?.apartments || [];
            const aptRows = [];
            Object.entries(aptMonthlyDetail).forEach(function(entry) {
                var aptId = entry[0], months = entry[1];
                var amd = months[monthKey];
                if (amd && (amd.gross > 0 || amd.resCount > 0)) {
                    var apt = apartments.find(function(a) { return a.id === aptId; });
                    aptRows.push({ id: aptId, code: apt ? apt.code : aptId, ownerShare: amd.ownerShare || 0, gross: amd.gross || 0, resCount: amd.resCount || 0, avgNightly: amd.avgNightly || 0 });
                }
            });
            aptRows.sort(function(a, b) { return b.ownerShare - a.ownerShare; });
            const maxAptOwner = aptRows.length > 0 ? Math.max(...aptRows.map(function(a) { return a.ownerShare; })) : 0;

            // Gelecek ve geçmiş rezervasyonlar (dedup ile)
            var futureRes = [], pastRes = [];
            var seenFuture = {}, seenPast = {};
            apartments.forEach(function(apt) {
                var fr = Projection.getPmsResForMonth(apt.id, m, y).concat(Projection.getAnalyticsResForMonth(apt.id, m, y));
                fr.forEach(function(r) {
                    r._aptCode = apt.code;
                    var key = (r.guestName || '') + '_' + r.checkIn + '_' + r.checkOut;
                    if (!seenFuture[key]) { seenFuture[key] = true; futureRes.push(r); }
                });
                var pr = Projection.getPmsResForMonth(apt.id, m, y - 1).concat(Projection.getAnalyticsResForMonth(apt.id, m, y - 1));
                pr.forEach(function(r) {
                    r._aptCode = apt.code;
                    var key = (r.guestName || '') + '_' + r.checkIn + '_' + r.checkOut;
                    if (!seenPast[key]) { seenPast[key] = true; pastRes.push(r); }
                });
            });

            // Geçmiş yıl istatistikleri
            var pastTotal = 0, pastNights = 0;
            pastRes.forEach(function(r) {
                var nights = r.nights || Math.max(1, Math.round((new Date(r.checkOut) - new Date(r.checkIn)) / 86400000));
                pastTotal += convertEUR(r.totalPrice ?? r.totalAmount ?? 0, r.currency || 'EUR');
                pastNights += nights;
            });
            var pastAvgNightly = pastNights > 0 ? pastTotal / pastNights : 0;
            var futureTotal = 0, futureNights = 0;
            futureRes.forEach(function(r) {
                var nights = r.nights || Math.max(1, Math.round((new Date(r.checkOut) - new Date(r.checkIn)) / 86400000));
                futureTotal += convertEUR(r.totalPrice ?? r.totalAmount ?? 0, r.currency || 'EUR');
                futureNights += nights;
            });
            var futureAvgNightly = futureNights > 0 ? futureTotal / futureNights : 0;

            // Yıllık değişim (ortalama gecelik ücret bazında)
            var yoyChange = pastAvgNightly > 0 && futureAvgNightly > 0 ? (((futureAvgNightly - pastAvgNightly) / pastAvgNightly) * 100) : null;

            // Modal HTML
            var html = '<div id="projResDetailOverlay" class="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4" onclick="if(event.target===this)this.remove()">';
            html += '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-w-2xl w-full max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">';

            // Header
            html += '<div class="bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-emerald-700 text-white p-5 rounded-t-2xl">';
            html += '<div class="flex justify-between items-start">';
            html += '<div><h3 class="text-lg font-bold">' + mName + '</h3>';
            html += '<p class="text-emerald-200 text-sm mt-0.5">' + t('proj.detail.title', 'Projeksiyon Detayı') + '</p></div>';
            html += '<button onclick="document.getElementById(\'projResDetailOverlay\').remove()" class="text-white/70 hover:text-white text-2xl leading-none">&times;</button>';
            html += '</div>';
            html += '<div class="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-emerald-500/30">';
            html += '<div class="text-emerald-200 text-xs">' + t('proj.est.net.income', 'Tahmini Net Kazancınız') + '</div>';
            html += '<div class="text-2xl font-bold">\u20ac' + ownerVal.toFixed(0) + '</div></div></div>';

            // Tabs
            html += '<div class="border-b border-gray-200 flex">';
            html += '<button class="ownerMonthTab flex-1 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-emerald-600 border-b-2 border-emerald-500" data-tab="summary" onclick="Projection._switchOwnerMonthTab(\'summary\')">' + t('proj.summary', 'Özet') + '</button>';
            html += '<button class="ownerMonthTab flex-1 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 border-b-2 border-transparent" data-tab="future" onclick="Projection._switchOwnerMonthTab(\'future\')">';
            html += t('proj.future.res', 'Gelecek Rez.') + ' <span class="text-[10px] bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full ml-1">' + futureRes.length + '</span></button>';
            html += '<button class="ownerMonthTab flex-1 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 border-b-2 border-transparent" data-tab="past" onclick="Projection._switchOwnerMonthTab(\'past\')">';
            html += t('proj.past.year', 'Geçmiş Yıl') + ' <span class="text-[10px] bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full ml-1">' + pastRes.length + '</span></button>';
            html += '</div>';

            // === Tab 1: Özet ===
            html += '<div id="ownerMonthPane_summary" class="p-5">';

            // KPI kartları
            html += '<div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 mb-4">';
            html += '<div class="bg-blue-50 rounded-lg p-3 text-center"><div class="text-[10px] text-blue-500 font-medium mb-1">' + t('proj.gross.income', 'Brüt Gelir') + '</div><div class="text-base font-bold text-blue-700">\u20ac' + (md.gross || 0).toFixed(0) + '</div></div>';
            html += '<div class="bg-emerald-50 rounded-lg p-3 text-center"><div class="text-[10px] text-emerald-500 font-medium mb-1">' + t('proj.your.net', 'Net Kazancınız') + '</div><div class="text-base font-bold text-emerald-700">\u20ac' + ownerVal.toFixed(0) + '</div></div>';
            html += '<div class="bg-purple-50 rounded-lg p-3 text-center"><div class="text-[10px] text-purple-500 font-medium mb-1">' + t('proj.avg.nightly', 'Ort. Gecelik') + '</div><div class="text-base font-bold text-purple-700">\u20ac' + (md.avgNightly || 0).toFixed(0) + '</div></div>';
            html += '</div>';

            // Kesinti detayları
            var mdPlatComm = md.platComm || 0;
            var mdLagirioComm = md.lagirio || 0;
            var mdKdv = md.kdv || 0;
            var mdExpenses = md.expenses || 0;
            html += '<div class="bg-gray-50 rounded-xl p-4 mb-5">';
            html += '<h4 class="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide mb-3">' + t('proj.deduction.detail', 'Kesinti Detayı') + '</h4>';
            html += '<div class="space-y-2">';
            var deductionItems = [
                { label: t('proj.platform.comm', 'Platform Komisyonu'), value: mdPlatComm, color: 'text-orange-600', bg: 'bg-orange-400' },
                { label: t('proj.lagirio.comm', 'Lagirio Komisyonu'), value: mdLagirioComm, color: 'text-indigo-600', bg: 'bg-indigo-400' },
                { label: t('proj.kdv.taxes', 'KDV / Vergiler'), value: mdKdv, color: 'text-red-600', bg: 'bg-red-400' },
                { label: t('proj.expenses', 'Giderler'), value: mdExpenses, color: 'text-amber-600', bg: 'bg-amber-400' }
            ];
            var maxDeduction = Math.max(mdPlatComm, mdLagirioComm, mdKdv, mdExpenses, 1);
            deductionItems.forEach(function(item) {
                var barW = maxDeduction > 0 ? Math.max(3, (item.value / maxDeduction) * 100) : 3;
                html += '<div class="flex items-center gap-3">';
                html += '<div class="w-32 text-[11px] font-medium text-gray-600">' + item.label + '</div>';
                html += '<div class="flex-1 bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-2"><div class="' + item.bg + ' h-2 rounded-full" style="width:' + barW.toFixed(1) + '%"></div></div>';
                html += '<div class="w-16 text-right text-[11px] font-bold ' + item.color + '">-\u20ac' + item.value.toFixed(0) + '</div>';
                html += '</div>';
            });
            html += '<div class="border-t border-gray-200 mt-2 pt-2 flex justify-between">';
            html += '<span class="text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-600">' + t('proj.total.deduction', 'Toplam Kesinti') + '</span>';
            html += '<span class="text-[11px] font-bold text-red-600">-\u20ac' + deductions.toFixed(0) + '</span>';
            html += '</div></div></div>';

            // Daire dağılımı
            if (aptRows.length > 0) {
                html += '<h4 class="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide mb-3">' + t('proj.apt.breakdown', 'Daire Bazlı Dağılım') + '</h4>';
                html += '<div class="space-y-2.5 mb-4">';
                aptRows.forEach(function(a) {
                    var barW = maxAptOwner > 0 ? Math.max(5, (a.ownerShare / maxAptOwner) * 100) : 5;
                    html += '<div>';
                    html += '<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">';
                    html += '<span class="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">' + a.code + '</span>';
                    html += '<div class="flex items-center gap-3 text-[11px]">';
                    html += '<span class="text-gray-400">' + a.resCount + ' rez</span>';
                    html += '<span class="text-gray-400">\u20ac' + a.avgNightly.toFixed(0) + '/g</span>';
                    html += '<span class="font-bold text-emerald-600">\u20ac' + a.ownerShare.toFixed(0) + '</span>';
                    html += '</div></div>';
                    html += '<div class="w-full bg-gray-100 rounded-full h-2"><div class="bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-400 to-emerald-500 h-2 rounded-full" style="width:' + barW + '%"></div></div>';
                    html += '</div>';
                });
                html += '</div>';
            }

            // Yıllık karşılaştırma mini
            if (yoyChange !== null) {
                var changeClass = yoyChange >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50 border-emerald-200' : 'text-red-600 bg-red-50 border-red-200';
                var arrow = yoyChange >= 0 ? '\u2191' : '\u2193';
                html += '<div class="border ' + changeClass + ' rounded-lg p-3 flex items-center justify-between">';
                html += '<span class="text-xs text-gray-600">' + t('proj.avg.nightly.rate', 'Ort. gecelik ücret değişimi') + ' <span class="text-gray-400">(\u20ac' + pastAvgNightly.toFixed(0) + ' \u2192 \u20ac' + futureAvgNightly.toFixed(0) + ')</span></span>';
                html += '<span class="text-sm font-bold">' + arrow + ' %' + Math.abs(yoyChange).toFixed(1) + '</span>';
                html += '</div>';
            }
            html += '</div>';

            // === Tab 2: Gelecek Rezervasyonlar ===
            html += '<div id="ownerMonthPane_future" class="p-5 hidden">';
            if (futureRes.length > 0) {
                html += '<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs"><thead class="bg-gray-50"><tr>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-left font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.th.apt', 'Daire') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-left font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.guest', 'Misafir') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-center font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.date', 'Tarih') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-center font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('common.night', 'Gece') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-left font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.platform', 'Platform') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.amount', 'Tutar') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.per.night', '/Gece') + '</th>';
                html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';
                futureRes.forEach(function(res) {
                    var nights = res.nights || Math.max(1, Math.round((new Date(res.checkOut) - new Date(res.checkIn)) / 86400000));
                    var amount = convertEUR(res.totalPrice ?? res.totalAmount ?? 0, res.currency || 'EUR');
                    var perNight = nights > 0 ? amount / nights : 0;
                    var platformColor = (res.platform === 'Airbnb') ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-600' : (res.platform === 'Booking.com') ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700' : 'bg-green-50 text-green-600';
                    html += '<tr class="border-t border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 font-medium text-gray-600">' + (res._aptCode || '-') + '</td>';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-gray-700">' + (res.guestName || '-') + '</td>';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-center text-gray-600 text-[11px]">' + fmt(res.checkIn) + ' \u2192 ' + fmt(res.checkOut) + '</td>';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-center font-semibold">' + nights + '</td>';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3"><span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ' + platformColor + '">' + (res.platform || '-') + '</span></td>';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium">\u20ac' + amount.toFixed(2) + '</td>';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-gray-500">\u20ac' + perNight.toFixed(2) + '</td>';
                    html += '</tr>';
                });
                html += '<tr class="border-t-2 border-gray-300 font-bold bg-gray-50">';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3" colspan="3">' + t('proj.total', 'TOPLAM') + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-center">' + futureNights + '</td><td></td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-emerald-600">\u20ac' + futureTotal.toFixed(2) + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-gray-500">\u20ac' + futureAvgNightly.toFixed(2) + '</td>';
                html += '</tr></tbody></table></div>';
                html += '<div class="flex gap-3 mt-3">';
                html += '<div class="bg-emerald-50 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs"><span class="text-gray-500">' + t('proj.avg.nightly.colon', 'Ort. Gecelik:') + '</span> <strong class="text-emerald-700">\u20ac' + futureAvgNightly.toFixed(2) + '</strong></div>';
                html += '<div class="bg-blue-50 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs"><span class="text-gray-500">' + t('proj.total.nights', 'Toplam Gece:') + '</span> <strong class="text-blue-700">' + futureNights + '</strong></div>';
                html += '<div class="bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs"><span class="text-gray-500">' + t('proj.res.count', 'Rez. Sayısı:') + '</span> <strong class="text-amber-700">' + futureRes.length + '</strong></div>';
                html += '</div>';
            } else {
                html += '<div class="text-center py-8"><i data-lucide="calendar-x" class="w-8 h-8 text-gray-300 mx-auto mb-2"></i>';
                html += '<p class="text-sm text-gray-400">' + t('proj.no.future.res', 'Bu ay için henüz gelecek rezervasyon yok') + '</p>';
                html += '<p class="text-xs text-gray-300 mt-1">' + t('proj.est.from.past', 'Tahminler geçmiş verilerden üretilmiştir') + '</p></div>';
            }
            html += '</div>';

            // === Tab 3: Geçmiş Yıl ===
            html += '<div id="ownerMonthPane_past" class="p-5 hidden">';
            if (pastRes.length > 0) {
                html += '<div class="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg p-3 mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">';
                html += '<span class="text-xs text-amber-700">' + monthNames[m] + ' ' + (y - 1) + ' ' + t('proj.data.suffix', 'verileri') + '</span>';
                html += '<span class="text-sm font-bold text-amber-700">' + pastRes.length + ' ' + t('common.reservation', 'rezervasyon') + '</span>';
                html += '</div>';
                html += '<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs"><thead class="bg-gray-50"><tr>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-left font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.th.apt', 'Daire') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-left font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.guest', 'Misafir') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-center font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.date', 'Tarih') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-center font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('common.night', 'Gece') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-left font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.platform', 'Platform') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.amount', 'Tutar') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.per.night', '/Gece') + '</th>';
                html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';
                pastRes.forEach(function(res) {
                    var nights = res.nights || Math.max(1, Math.round((new Date(res.checkOut) - new Date(res.checkIn)) / 86400000));
                    var amount = convertEUR(res.totalPrice ?? res.totalAmount ?? 0, res.currency || 'EUR');
                    var perNight = nights > 0 ? amount / nights : 0;
                    var platformColor = (res.platform === 'Airbnb') ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-600' : (res.platform === 'Booking.com') ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700' : 'bg-green-50 text-green-600';
                    html += '<tr class="border-t border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 font-medium text-gray-600">' + (res._aptCode || '-') + '</td>';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-gray-700">' + (res.guestName || '-') + '</td>';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-center text-gray-600 text-[11px]">' + fmt(res.checkIn) + ' \u2192 ' + fmt(res.checkOut) + '</td>';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-center font-semibold">' + nights + '</td>';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3"><span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ' + platformColor + '">' + (res.platform || '-') + '</span></td>';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-medium">\u20ac' + amount.toFixed(2) + '</td>';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-gray-500">\u20ac' + perNight.toFixed(2) + '</td>';
                    html += '</tr>';
                });
                html += '<tr class="border-t-2 border-gray-300 font-bold bg-gray-50">';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3" colspan="3">' + t('proj.total', 'TOPLAM') + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-center">' + pastNights + '</td><td></td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-amber-600">\u20ac' + pastTotal.toFixed(2) + '</td>';
                html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-right text-gray-500">\u20ac' + pastAvgNightly.toFixed(2) + '</td>';
                html += '</tr></tbody></table></div>';
                html += '<div class="flex gap-3 mt-3">';
                html += '<div class="bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs"><span class="text-gray-500">' + t('proj.avg.nightly.colon', 'Ort. Gecelik:') + '</span> <strong class="text-amber-700">\u20ac' + pastAvgNightly.toFixed(2) + '</strong></div>';
                html += '<div class="bg-blue-50 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs"><span class="text-gray-500">' + t('proj.total.nights', 'Toplam Gece:') + '</span> <strong class="text-blue-700">' + pastNights + '</strong></div>';
                html += '<div class="bg-gray-50 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs"><span class="text-gray-500">' + t('proj.res.count', 'Rez. Sayısı:') + '</span> <strong class="text-gray-700">' + pastRes.length + '</strong></div>';
                html += '</div>';
            } else {
                html += '<div class="text-center py-8"><i data-lucide="archive-x" class="w-8 h-8 text-gray-300 mx-auto mb-2"></i>';
                html += '<p class="text-sm text-gray-400">' + t('proj.no.past.data', 'Geçmiş yıl için bu ayda veri bulunamadı') + '</p>';
                html += '<p class="text-xs text-gray-300 mt-1">' + monthNames[m] + ' ' + (y - 1) + ' — ' + t('proj.no.data.label', 'veri yok') + '</p></div>';
            }
            html += '</div>';

            // Footer
            html += '<div class="p-4 border-t border-gray-100 flex justify-end">';
            html += '<button onclick="document.getElementById(\'projResDetailOverlay\').remove()" class="bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition">' + t('proj.close', 'Kapat') + '</button>';
            html += '</div></div></div>';

            document.getElementById('projResDetailOverlay')?.remove();
            document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html);
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        },

        _switchOwnerMonthTab(tabName) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.ownerMonthTab').forEach(function(btn) {
                var isActive = btn.dataset.tab === tabName;
                btn.className = 'ownerMonthTab flex-1 py-3 text-sm font-medium ' + (isActive ? 'text-emerald-600 border-b-2 border-emerald-500' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 border-b-2 border-transparent');
            });
            ['summary', 'future', 'past'].forEach(function(t) {
                var pane = document.getElementById('ownerMonthPane_' + t);
                if (pane) pane.classList.toggle('hidden', t !== tabName);
            });
        },

        // ========= OWNER: Ay Bazlı Rezervasyon Paneli (gelecek + geçmiş yıl) =========
        renderOwnerFutureReservations(el) {
            var self = this;
            var convertEUR = Analytics.convertToEUR ? Analytics.convertToEUR.bind(Analytics) : function(a) { return a; };
            var fmt = function(d) { var dt = new Date(d); return dt.getDate() + '.' + (dt.getMonth()+1) + '.' + dt.getFullYear(); };
            var monthNames = I18n.getMonthNames('full');
            var apartments = Analytics?.data?.apartments || [];

            if (apartments.length === 0) {
                el.innerHTML = '<p class="text-sm text-gray-400 text-center py-4">' + t('proj.no.apt.data', 'Daire verisi yok') + '</p>';
                return;
            }

            // 12 aylık periyot oluştur
            var now = new Date();
            var startMonth = now.getMonth();
            var startYear = now.getFullYear();
            var months12 = [];
            for (var i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
                var mm = startMonth + i, yy = startYear;
                if (mm >= 12) { mm -= 12; yy++; }
                months12.push({ month: mm, year: yy, monthKey: yy + '-' + String(mm + 1).padStart(2, '0') });
            }

            // Her ay için gelecek + geçmiş rez topla (dedup ile)
            var monthsData = [];
            months12.forEach(function(mp) {
                var futureRes = [], pastRes = [];
                var seenF = {}, seenP = {};
                apartments.forEach(function(apt) {
                    // Gelecek (bu yıl): PMS + Analytics
                    var fr = self.getPmsResForMonth(apt.id, mp.month, mp.year)
                        .concat(self.getAnalyticsResForMonth(apt.id, mp.month, mp.year));
                    fr.forEach(function(r) {
                        r._aptCode = apt.code;
                        var key = (r.guestName || '') + '_' + r.checkIn + '_' + r.checkOut;
                        if (!seenF[key]) { seenF[key] = true; futureRes.push(r); }
                    });
                    // Geçmiş yıl (aynı ay, bir önceki yıl)
                    var pr = self.getPmsResForMonth(apt.id, mp.month, mp.year - 1)
                        .concat(self.getAnalyticsResForMonth(apt.id, mp.month, mp.year - 1));
                    pr.forEach(function(r) {
                        r._aptCode = apt.code;
                        var key = (r.guestName || '') + '_' + r.checkIn + '_' + r.checkOut;
                        if (!seenP[key]) { seenP[key] = true; pastRes.push(r); }
                    });
                });
                futureRes.sort(function(a, b) { return new Date(a.checkIn) - new Date(b.checkIn); });
                pastRes.sort(function(a, b) { return new Date(a.checkIn) - new Date(b.checkIn); });

                // Toplamlar
                var fTotal = 0, fNights = 0, pTotal = 0, pNights = 0;
                futureRes.forEach(function(r) {
                    var n = r.nights || Math.max(1, Math.round((new Date(r.checkOut) - new Date(r.checkIn)) / 86400000));
                    fTotal += convertEUR(r.totalPrice ?? r.totalAmount ?? 0, r.currency || 'EUR');
                    fNights += n;
                });
                pastRes.forEach(function(r) {
                    var n = r.nights || Math.max(1, Math.round((new Date(r.checkOut) - new Date(r.checkIn)) / 86400000));
                    pTotal += convertEUR(r.totalPrice ?? r.totalAmount ?? 0, r.currency || 'EUR');
                    pNights += n;
                });

                monthsData.push({
                    mk: mp.monthKey, month: mp.month, year: mp.year,
                    mName: monthNames[mp.month] + ' ' + mp.year,
                    futureRes: futureRes, pastRes: pastRes,
                    fTotal: fTotal, fNights: fNights, pTotal: pTotal, pNights: pNights
                });
            });

            // Toplam rez sayısı
            var totalFuture = 0, totalPast = 0;
            monthsData.forEach(function(md) { totalFuture += md.futureRes.length; totalPast += md.pastRes.length; });

            var html = '<div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">';
            html += '<div class="text-sm text-gray-500">';
            html += '<span class="text-emerald-600 font-semibold">' + totalFuture + '</span> ' + t('proj.future', 'gelecek');
            html += ' · <span class="text-amber-600 font-semibold">' + totalPast + '</span> ' + t('proj.past.year.short', 'geçmiş yıl');
            html += ' · ' + apartments.length + ' ' + t('common.apartment', 'daire') + ' · 12 ' + t('proj.col.month', 'ay') + '</div>';
            html += '</div>';

            html += '<div class="space-y-2">';

            // Rez satır render helper
            function renderResRow(r, isCompact) {
                var nights = r.nights || Math.max(1, Math.round((new Date(r.checkOut) - new Date(r.checkIn)) / 86400000));
                var amount = convertEUR(r.totalPrice ?? r.totalAmount ?? 0, r.currency || 'EUR');
                var perNight = nights > 0 ? amount / nights : 0;
                var platformColor = (r.platform === 'Airbnb') ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-600' : (r.platform === 'Booking.com') ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700' : 'bg-green-50 text-green-600';
                var s = '<div class="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-1.5 hover:bg-gray-50">';
                s += '<div class="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">';
                if (apartments.length > 1) s += '<span class="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 shrink-0">' + (r._aptCode || '-') + '</span>';
                s += '<span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[9px] font-bold ' + platformColor + ' shrink-0">' + (r.platform || '-') + '</span>';
                s += '<span class="text-[11px] text-gray-700 truncate">' + (r.guestName || t('proj.col.guest', 'Misafir')) + '</span>';
                s += '<span class="text-[10px] text-gray-400 shrink-0">' + fmt(r.checkIn) + '\u2192' + fmt(r.checkOut) + '</span>';
                s += '</div>';
                s += '<div class="text-right shrink-0 ml-2"><span class="text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-800">\u20ac' + amount.toFixed(0) + '</span>';
                s += '<span class="text-[9px] text-gray-400 ml-1">' + nights + 'g</span></div>';
                s += '</div>';
                return s;
            }

            monthsData.forEach(function(md, idx) {
                var hasData = md.futureRes.length > 0 || md.pastRes.length > 0;
                var borderColor = md.futureRes.length > 0 ? 'border-emerald-200' : md.pastRes.length > 0 ? 'border-gray-200' : 'border-gray-100';

                html += '<div class="border ' + borderColor + ' rounded-lg overflow-hidden">';

                // Ay başlığı
                html += '<div class="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-2.5 bg-gray-50 cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors" onclick="Projection._toggleOwnerMonthRes(\'' + md.mk + '\')">';
                html += '<div class="flex items-center gap-2">';
                html += '<i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-gray-400 transition-transform" id="ownerResChev_' + md.mk + '"' + (idx === 0 && hasData ? ' style="transform:rotate(90deg)"' : '') + '></i>';
                html += '<span class="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700">' + md.mName + '</span>';
                if (md.futureRes.length > 0) {
                    html += '<span class="text-[10px] bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold">' + md.futureRes.length + ' ' + t('proj.res.badge', 'rez') + '</span>';
                }
                if (md.pastRes.length > 0) {
                    html += '<span class="text-[10px] bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold">' + md.pastRes.length + ' ' + t('proj.past.label', 'geçmiş') + '</span>';
                }
                if (!hasData) {
                    html += '<span class="text-[10px] text-gray-300">' + t('proj.no.data.label', 'veri yok') + '</span>';
                }
                html += '</div>';

                // Sağ taraf: ort. gecelik ücret karşılaştırması
                var fAvgNightly = md.fNights > 0 ? md.fTotal / md.fNights : 0;
                var pAvgNightly = md.pNights > 0 ? md.pTotal / md.pNights : 0;
                html += '<div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">';
                if (fAvgNightly > 0) {
                    html += '<span class="text-emerald-600 font-bold">\u20ac' + fAvgNightly.toFixed(0) + '/g</span>';
                }
                if (pAvgNightly > 0) {
                    html += '<span class="text-amber-500">\u20ac' + pAvgNightly.toFixed(0) + '/g</span>';
                }
                if (fAvgNightly > 0 && pAvgNightly > 0) {
                    var change = ((fAvgNightly - pAvgNightly) / pAvgNightly * 100);
                    var changeClass = change >= 0 ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-red-500';
                    html += '<span class="text-[10px] font-bold ' + changeClass + '">' + (change >= 0 ? '\u2191' : '\u2193') + Math.abs(change).toFixed(0) + '%</span>';
                }
                html += '</div></div>';

                // İçerik
                html += '<div id="ownerResBody_' + md.mk + '" class="' + (idx === 0 && hasData ? '' : 'hidden') + '">';

                // Gelecek rezervasyonlar
                if (md.futureRes.length > 0) {
                    html += '<div class="border-l-2 border-emerald-300 ml-3">';
                    html += '<div class="px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-600 uppercase tracking-wide bg-emerald-50/50">';
                    html += t('proj.current.future', 'Mevcut / Gelecek Rezervasyonlar') + ' (' + md.futureRes.length + ')';
                    html += ' \u2014 ' + md.fNights + ' ' + t('common.night', 'gece') + ' \u2014 \u20ac' + md.fTotal.toFixed(0);
                    html += '</div>';
                    html += '<div class="divide-y divide-gray-50">';
                    md.futureRes.forEach(function(r) { html += renderResRow(r); });
                    html += '</div></div>';
                }

                // Geçmiş yıl
                if (md.pastRes.length > 0) {
                    html += '<div class="border-l-2 border-amber-300 ml-3">';
                    html += '<div class="px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-600 uppercase tracking-wide bg-amber-50/50">';
                    html += t('proj.past.year.label', 'Geçmiş Yıl') + ' \u2014 ' + monthNames[md.month] + ' ' + (md.year - 1);
                    html += ' (' + md.pastRes.length + ')';
                    html += ' \u2014 ' + md.pNights + ' ' + t('common.night', 'gece') + ' \u2014 \u20ac' + md.pTotal.toFixed(0);
                    html += '</div>';
                    html += '<div class="divide-y divide-gray-50">';
                    md.pastRes.forEach(function(r) { html += renderResRow(r); });
                    html += '</div></div>';
                }

                if (!hasData) {
                    html += '<div class="px-4 py-3 text-xs text-gray-400 text-center">' + t('proj.no.res.data', 'Bu ay için rezervasyon verisi yok') + '</div>';
                }

                html += '</div></div>';
            });

            html += '</div>';
            el.innerHTML = html;
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        },

        _toggleOwnerMonthRes(mk) {
            var body = document.getElementById('ownerResBody_' + mk);
            var chev = document.getElementById('ownerResChev_' + mk);
            if (!body) return;
            var isHidden = body.classList.contains('hidden');
            body.classList.toggle('hidden');
            if (chev) chev.style.transform = isHidden ? 'rotate(90deg)' : '';
        },

        renderApartmentList(results, aptMonthlyDetail) {
            const el = document.getElementById('projApartmentList');
            if (!el) return;
            const isOwner = Auth.currentUser?.role === 'owner';
            this._aptMonthlyDetail = aptMonthlyDetail || {};

            if (isOwner) {
                // Owner: Daire accordion yerine ay bazlı rez paneli (gelecek + geçmiş yıl)
                const titleEl = document.getElementById('projAptSectionTitle');
                const subtitleEl = document.getElementById('projAptSectionSubtitle');
                if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = t('proj.reservations', 'Rezervasyonlar');
                if (subtitleEl) subtitleEl.textContent = t('proj.12m.compare', '(12 ay — gelecek + geçmiş yıl karşılaştırmalı)');
                this.renderOwnerFutureReservations(el);
                return;
            }

            const sorted = results.sort((a, b) => b.lagirio - a.lagirio);
            const sysNames = { '1': 'S1 Net', '2': 'S2 Br\u00fct', '3': 'S3 Kar', '4': 'S4 Plat', '5': 'S5 Stop', '6': 'S6 Net', '7': 'S7 Karma', '8': 'S8 Kar', '9': 'S9 Stop-KDV' };
            const monthNames = I18n.getMonthNames('short');
            const sourceColorMap = { emerald: 'bg-emerald-50 border-l-2 border-emerald-400', amber: 'bg-amber-50 border-l-2 border-amber-400', blue: 'bg-blue-50 border-l-2 border-blue-400', orange: 'bg-orange-50 border-l-2 border-orange-400', red: 'bg-red-50 border-l-2 border-red-300' };

            // Owner i\u00e7in gelecek rez say\u0131s\u0131 hesapla
            const today = new Date(); today.setHours(0,0,0,0);
            const allReservations = this.reservations || [];

            el.innerHTML = sorted.map((apt, i) => {
                const detail = this._aptMonthlyDetail[apt.id] || {};
                const months = Object.keys(detail).sort();

                // Bu dairenin gelecek rez say\u0131s\u0131
                const futureResForApt = allReservations.filter(r => r.apartmentId === apt.id && new Date(r.checkIn) >= today);
                const futureResCount = futureResForApt.length;

                let monthRows = '';
                if (months.length > 0) {
                    monthRows = months.map(mk => {
                        const d = detail[mk];
                        const [y, m] = mk.split('-');
                        const mName = monthNames[parseInt(m)-1] + ' ' + y;
                        const rowClass = sourceColorMap[d.sourceColor] || '';
                        const adjustInput = d.needsAdjustment
                            ? ' <input type="number" value="' + (d.adjustPct || 0) + '" style="width:45px;font-size:10px;padding:1px 3px;border:1px solid #ddd;border-radius:3px;text-align:center;" onchange="Projection.updateChainAdjust(\'' + d.adjustKey + '\', this.value)" title="% ayarlama">%'
                            : '';
                        const nightsInfo = d.totalNights > 0 ? d.totalNights + t('proj.day.abbr', 'g') : '-';
                        const isClickable = d.resCount > 0 && d.source !== 'veri-yok';
                        const clickAttr = isClickable ? ' onclick="event.stopPropagation(); Projection.showReservationDetail(\'' + apt.id + '\',' + (parseInt(m)-1) + ',' + y + ')" style="cursor:pointer;" title="' + t('proj.detail.click', 'Rezervasyon detayı için tıklayın') + '"' : '';

                        if (isOwner) {
                            const ownerVal = d.ownerShare || 0;
                            const deduction = (d.gross || 0) - ownerVal;
                            // Belirgin rez badge'i
                            const rezBadgeColor = d.sourceColor === 'emerald' ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200' : d.sourceColor === 'amber' ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200' : d.sourceColor === 'orange' ? 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-700 border border-orange-200' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 border border-gray-200';
                            const rezBadge = isClickable
                                ? '<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ' + rezBadgeColor + ' cursor-pointer hover:shadow-sm transition-shadow"><i data-lucide="calendar-check" class="w-3 h-3"></i>' + d.resCount + ' ' + t('proj.view.res', 'rez görüntüle') + '</span>'
                                : '<span class="text-[10px] text-gray-400">' + d.resCount + ' ' + t('proj.res.badge', 'rez') + '</span>';

                            return '<tr class="border-t border-gray-100 ' + rowClass + (isClickable ? ' hover:bg-emerald-50/50 transition-colors' : '') + '"' + clickAttr + '>' +
                                '<td class="py-2 px-2 text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">' + mName + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-2 px-2">' + rezBadge + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-2 px-2 text-[10px] text-gray-400">' + (d.sourceLabel || '') + adjustInput + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-2 px-2 text-xs text-right font-medium">\u20ac' + d.gross.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-2 px-2 text-xs text-right text-red-500">-\u20ac' + deduction.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-2 px-2 text-xs text-right font-bold text-emerald-600">\u20ac' + ownerVal.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '</tr>';
                        } else {
                            return '<tr class="border-t border-gray-100 ' + rowClass + (isClickable ? ' hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors' : '') + '"' + clickAttr + '>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs font-medium text-gray-600">' + mName + (isClickable ? ' <i data-lucide="search" class="w-3 h-3 inline text-gray-300"></i>' : '') + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-[10px] text-gray-400">' + (d.sourceLabel || '') + adjustInput + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right">' + d.resCount + ' <span class="text-gray-300">(' + nightsInfo + ')</span></td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right">\u20ac' + d.avgNightly.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right font-medium">\u20ac' + d.gross.toFixed(2) + ' <span class="text-[9px] text-gray-300">\u00d7' + Projection.OCCUPANCY_DAYS + 'g</span></td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right text-orange-500">\u20ac' + d.expenses.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right text-red-500">\u20ac' + d.kdv.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right text-indigo-500">\u20ac' + d.platComm.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right font-bold text-emerald-600">\u20ac' + d.lagirio.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                                '</tr>';
                        }
                    }).join('');
                }

                // Detail table headers
                let detailHeaders, detailTotalRow;
                if (isOwner) {
                    const ownerTotal = apt.ownerShare || 0;
                    const totalDeduction = (apt.gross || 0) - ownerTotal;
                    detailHeaders = '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-left">' + t('proj.col.month', 'Ay') + '</th>' +
                        '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-left">' + t('proj.col.res', 'Rez.') + '</th>' +
                        '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-left">' + t('proj.col.source', 'Kaynak') + '</th>' +
                        '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right">' + t('proj.gross', 'Brüt') + '</th>' +
                        '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right">' + t('proj.deduction', 'Kesinti') + '</th>' +
                        '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right">' + t('proj.net', 'Net') + '</th>';
                    detailTotalRow = '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs" colspan="3">' + t('proj.total.12m', 'TOPLAM (12 Ay)') + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right">\u20ac' + apt.gross.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right text-red-500">-\u20ac' + totalDeduction.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right text-emerald-600">\u20ac' + ownerTotal.toFixed(2) + '</td>';
                } else {
                    detailHeaders = '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-left">' + t('proj.col.month', 'Ay') + '</th>' +
                        '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-left">' + t('proj.col.source', 'Kaynak') + '</th>' +
                        '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right">' + t('proj.col.nights.res', 'Rez (Gece)') + '</th>' +
                        '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right">' + t('proj.col.per.night', '/Gece') + '</th>' +
                        '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right">' + t('proj.col.gross.25d', 'Brüt (×25g)') + '</th>' +
                        '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right">' + t('proj.col.apt.expense', 'Gider') + '</th>' +
                        '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right">' + t('proj.col.vat', 'KDV') + '</th>' +
                        '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right">' + t('proj.col.plt.comm', 'Plt.Kom') + '</th>' +
                        '<th class="py-1 px-2 text-right">' + t('proj.col.lagirio', 'Lagirio') + '</th>';
                    detailTotalRow = '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs" colspan="2">' + t('proj.total.12m', 'TOPLAM (12 Ay)') + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right">' + apt.count + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right">\u20ac' + apt.avgNightly.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right">\u20ac' + apt.gross.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right text-orange-500">\u20ac' + apt.expenses.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right text-red-500">\u20ac' + apt.kdv.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right text-indigo-500">\u20ac' + apt.platComm.toFixed(2) + '</td>' +
                        '<td class="py-1.5 px-2 text-xs text-right text-emerald-600">\u20ac' + apt.lagirio.toFixed(2) + '</td>';
                }

                // Owner i\u00e7in inline rez \u00f6zeti - accordion a\u00e7\u0131ld\u0131\u011f\u0131nda g\u00f6r\u00fcn\u00fcr
                let ownerInlineResSection = '';
                if (isOwner && futureResForApt.length > 0) {
                    const convertEUR = Analytics.convertToEUR ? Analytics.convertToEUR.bind(Analytics) : (a) => a;
                    const fmt = d => { const dt = new Date(d); return dt.getDate() + '.' + (dt.getMonth()+1) + '.' + dt.getFullYear(); };
                    const upcoming = futureResForApt
                        .sort((a, b) => new Date(a.checkIn) - new Date(b.checkIn))
                        .slice(0, 5);

                    ownerInlineResSection = '<div class="mt-3 mb-2">' +
                        '<div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">' +
                        '<i data-lucide="calendar-clock" class="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500"></i>' +
                        '<span class="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700">' + t('proj.upcoming.res', 'Yaklaşan Rezervasyonlar') + '</span>' +
                        '<span class="text-[10px] bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold">' + futureResForApt.length + ' ' + t('proj.res.badge', 'rez') + '</span>' +
                        '</div>' +
                        '<div class="space-y-1.5">';

                    upcoming.forEach(function(res) {
                        const nights = res.nights || Math.max(1, Math.round((new Date(res.checkOut) - new Date(res.checkIn)) / 86400000));
                        const amount = res.totalPrice ?? res.totalAmount ?? 0;
                        const currency = res.currency || 'EUR';
                        const amountEUR = convertEUR(amount, currency);
                        const platformColor = (res.platform === 'Airbnb') ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-600 border-red-200' : (res.platform === 'Booking.com') ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-200' : 'bg-green-50 text-green-600 border-green-200';

                        ownerInlineResSection += '<div class="flex items-center justify-between bg-white border border-gray-100 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 hover:shadow-sm transition-shadow">' +
                            '<div class="flex items-center gap-3">' +
                            '<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold border ' + platformColor + '">' + (res.platform || '-') + '</span>' +
                            '<div>' +
                            '<span class="text-xs font-medium text-gray-700">' + (res.guestName || t('proj.col.guest', 'Misafir')) + '</span>' +
                            '<span class="text-[10px] text-gray-400 ml-2">' + fmt(res.checkIn) + ' \u2192 ' + fmt(res.checkOut) + ' (' + nights + 'g)</span>' +
                            '</div></div>' +
                            '<span class="text-xs font-bold text-gray-800">\u20ac' + amountEUR.toFixed(2) + '</span>' +
                            '</div>';
                    });

                    if (futureResForApt.length > 5) {
                        ownerInlineResSection += '<button onclick="event.stopPropagation(); Projection.showAllFutureResForApt(\'' + apt.id + '\')" class="w-full text-center py-1.5 text-[11px] text-emerald-600 hover:text-emerald-800 font-medium hover:bg-emerald-50 rounded-lg transition">' +
                            '+ ' + (futureResForApt.length - 5) + ' ' + t('proj.more.res', 'rez daha görüntüle') + '</button>';
                    }
                    ownerInlineResSection += '</div></div>';
                } else if (isOwner) {
                    ownerInlineResSection = '<div class="mt-3 mb-2 text-center py-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg">' +
                        '<i data-lucide="calendar-x" class="w-5 h-5 text-gray-300 mx-auto mb-1"></i>' +
                        '<p class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + t('proj.no.future.res', 'Henüz gelecek rezervasyon yok') + '</p>' +
                        '<p class="text-[10px] text-gray-300 mt-0.5">' + t('proj.est.from.past', 'Tahminler geçmiş verilerden üretilmektedir') + '</p>' +
                        '</div>';
                }

                const detailTable = '<div id="projAptDetail_' + i + '" class="hidden mt-2 mb-1 mx-1">' +
                    '<div class="bg-gray-50 rounded-lg p-3 overflow-x-auto">' +
                    ownerInlineResSection +
                    '<table class="w-full text-xs">' +
                    '<thead><tr class="text-gray-400 text-[10px] uppercase">' + detailHeaders +
                    '</tr></thead><tbody>' + monthRows +
                    '<tr class="border-t-2 border-gray-300 font-bold">' + detailTotalRow +
                    '</tr></tbody></table></div></div>';

                // Summary line for each apartment
                const mainValue = isOwner ? (apt.ownerShare || 0) : apt.lagirio;
                const summaryLine = isOwner
                    ? t('proj.summary.brut', 'Brüt') + ' \u20ac' + apt.gross.toFixed(2) + ' | ' + t('proj.summary.deduction', 'Kesinti') + ' -\u20ac' + ((apt.gross || 0) - (apt.ownerShare || 0)).toFixed(2)
                    : t('proj.summary.brut', 'Brüt') + ' \u20ac' + apt.gross.toFixed(2) + ' | ' + t('proj.summary.expense', 'Gdr') + ' \u20ac' + apt.expenses.toFixed(2) + ' | ' + t('proj.col.vat', 'KDV') + ' \u20ac' + apt.kdv.toFixed(2) + ' | ' + t('proj.col.plt.comm', 'Plt') + ' \u20ac' + apt.platComm.toFixed(2);

                // Owner: gelecek rez badge
                const futureResBadge = (isOwner && futureResCount > 0)
                    ? '<span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 ml-2"><i data-lucide="calendar-days" class="w-3 h-3"></i>' + futureResCount + ' ' + t('proj.upcoming.res', 'gelecek rez') + '</span>'
                    : '';

                return '<div class="border border-gray-100 rounded-lg overflow-hidden">' +
                    '<div class="flex items-center justify-between py-2.5 px-3 cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors" onclick="Projection.toggleAptDetail(' + i + ')">' +
                    '<div class="flex items-center gap-2">' +
                    '<span class="text-xs font-bold text-gray-300 w-5">' + (i+1) + '</span>' +
                    '<i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-gray-400 transition-transform" id="projAptChev_' + i + '"></i>' +
                    '<div><p class="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700">' + apt.code + futureResBadge + '</p>' +
                    '<p class="text-[10px] text-gray-400">' + (isOwner ? '' : (sysNames[apt.systemType] || 'S?') + ' | %' + apt.commission + ' | ') + apt.months + ' ' + t('proj.col.month', 'ay') + ' | \u20ac' + apt.avgNightly.toFixed(2) + '/g</p></div></div>' +
                    '<div class="text-right">' +
                    '<p class="text-sm font-bold text-emerald-600">\u20ac' + mainValue.toFixed(2) + '</p>' +
                    '<p class="text-[10px] text-gray-400">' + summaryLine + '</p></div></div>' +
                    detailTable + '</div>';
            }).join('');
            if (typeof lucide !== 'undefined') lucide.createIcons();
        },

        toggleAptDetail(idx) {
            const detail = document.getElementById('projAptDetail_' + idx);
            const chev = document.getElementById('projAptChev_' + idx);
            if (!detail) return;
            const isHidden = detail.classList.contains('hidden');
            detail.classList.toggle('hidden');
            if (chev) chev.style.transform = isHidden ? 'rotate(90deg)' : '';
        },

        showReservationDetail(aptId, month, year) {
            const isOwner = Auth.currentUser?.role === 'owner';
            const monthNamesFull = I18n.getMonthNames('full');
            const convertEUR = Analytics.convertToEUR ? Analytics.convertToEUR.bind(Analytics) : (a) => a;
            const apt = Analytics.data?.apartments?.find(a => a.id === aptId);
            const aptCode = apt ? apt.code : aptId;

            // Get reservations from all sources
            const futureFromPms = this.getPmsResForMonth(aptId, month, year);
            const futureFromAnalytics = this.getAnalyticsResForMonth(aptId, month, year);
            const pastFromPms = this.getPmsResForMonth(aptId, month, year - 1);
            const pastFromAnalytics = this.getAnalyticsResForMonth(aptId, month, year - 1);
            const futureRes = futureFromPms.concat(futureFromAnalytics);
            const pastRes = pastFromPms.concat(pastFromAnalytics);
            const hasFuture = futureRes.length > 0;
            const hasPast = pastRes.length > 0;

            let sections = [];
            if (hasFuture && !hasPast) {
                sections.push({ title: t('proj.current.res.label', 'Mevcut Rezervasyonlar') + ' (' + monthNamesFull[month] + ' ' + year + ')', reservations: futureRes, badge: t('proj.actual.data', 'Gerçek Veri'), badgeColor: 'emerald', note: null });
            } else if (!hasFuture && hasPast) {
                sections.push({ title: t('proj.past.year.res', 'Geçmiş Yıl Rezervasyonları') + ' (' + monthNamesFull[month] + ' ' + (year - 1) + ')', reservations: pastRes, badge: t('proj.past.plus5.label', 'Geçmiş Yıl +5%'), badgeColor: 'amber', note: t('proj.past.year.note', 'Bu ay için {0} yılında rezervasyon bulunmadığından, {1} yılı verileri %5 artışla kullanılmıştır.').replace('{0}', year).replace('{1}', year - 1) });
            } else if (hasFuture && hasPast) {
                sections.push({ title: t('proj.current.res.label', 'Mevcut Rezervasyonlar') + ' (' + monthNamesFull[month] + ' ' + year + ')', reservations: futureRes, badge: t('proj.future', 'Gelecek'), badgeColor: 'emerald', note: null });
                sections.push({ title: t('proj.past.year.res', 'Geçmiş Yıl Rezervasyonları') + ' (' + monthNamesFull[month] + ' ' + (year - 1) + ')', reservations: pastRes, badge: t('proj.past.year.label', 'Geçmiş Yıl'), badgeColor: 'amber', note: t('proj.both.year.note', 'Her iki yılın ortalaması alınarak hesaplanmıştır.') });
            } else {
                const chain = this.chainBackward(aptId, month, year);
                if (chain) {
                    const adjustKey = aptId + '_' + month + '_' + year;
                    const adjustPct = this.chainAdjustments[adjustKey] !== undefined ? this.chainAdjustments[adjustKey] : 0;
                    sections.push({ title: t('proj.chain.source', 'Zincir Kaynak:') + ' ' + monthNamesFull[chain.month] + ' ' + chain.year, reservations: chain.reservations, badge: t('proj.chain.label', 'Zincir') + ' ' + (adjustPct >= 0 ? '+' : '') + adjustPct + '%', badgeColor: 'orange', note: t('proj.chain.note', 'Bu ay için doğrudan veri bulunmadığından, en yakın veri kullanılmıştır.') });
                }
            }

            if (sections.length === 0) return;

            const badgeColorMap = { emerald: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700', amber: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700', orange: 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-700', blue: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700' };
            const borderColorMap = { emerald: 'border-emerald-400', amber: 'border-amber-400', orange: 'border-orange-400', blue: 'border-blue-400' };
            const fmt = d => { const dt = new Date(d); return dt.getDate() + '.' + (dt.getMonth()+1) + '.' + dt.getFullYear(); };

            // Owner i\u00e7in bu ay\u0131n ownerShare de\u011ferini hesapla
            let monthOwnerShare = 0;
            if (isOwner) {
                const monthKey = year + '-' + String(month + 1).padStart(2, '0');
                const aptDetail = this._aptMonthlyDetail?.[aptId];
                if (aptDetail && aptDetail[monthKey]) {
                    monthOwnerShare = aptDetail[monthKey].ownerShare || 0;
                }
            }

            let html = '<div id="projResDetailOverlay" class="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4" onclick="if(event.target===this)this.remove()">';
            html += '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-w-3xl w-full max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">';

            // Header
            if (isOwner) {
                html += '<div class="bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-emerald-700 text-white p-5 rounded-t-2xl">';
                html += '<div class="flex justify-between items-start">';
                html += '<div><h3 class="text-lg font-bold">' + aptCode + ' \u2014 ' + monthNamesFull[month] + ' ' + year + '</h3>';
                html += '<p class="text-emerald-100 text-sm mt-1">' + t('proj.res.details', 'Rezervasyon Detayları') + '</p></div>';
                html += '<button onclick="document.getElementById(\'projResDetailOverlay\').remove()" class="text-white/70 hover:text-white text-2xl leading-none">&times;</button>';
                html += '</div>';
                if (monthOwnerShare > 0) {
                    html += '<div class="mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-emerald-500/30 flex items-center gap-4">';
                    html += '<div><span class="text-emerald-200 text-xs">' + t('proj.month.est.net', 'Bu Aydaki Tahmini Net Kazancınız') + '</span>';
                    html += '<p class="text-xl font-bold">\u20ac' + monthOwnerShare.toFixed(2) + '</p></div>';
                    html += '</div>';
                }
                html += '</div>';
            } else {
                html += '<div class="bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-emerald-700 text-white p-5 rounded-t-2xl flex justify-between items-center">';
                html += '<div><h3 class="text-lg font-bold">' + aptCode + ' - ' + monthNamesFull[month] + ' ' + year + '</h3>';
                html += '<p class="text-emerald-100 text-sm mt-1">' + t('proj.calc.included.res', 'Hesaplamaya Dahil Edilen Rezervasyonlar') + '</p></div>';
                html += '<button onclick="document.getElementById(\'projResDetailOverlay\').remove()" class="text-white/70 hover:text-white text-2xl leading-none">&times;</button>';
                html += '</div>';
            }

            html += '<div class="p-5 space-y-6">';

            sections.forEach(function(section) {
                html += '<div>';
                html += '<div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">';
                html += '<h4 class="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">' + section.title + '</h4>';
                html += '<span class="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ' + (badgeColorMap[section.badgeColor] || 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600') + '">' + section.badge + '</span>';
                html += '</div>';

                if (section.note) {
                    html += '<p class="text-xs text-gray-500 bg-gray-50 p-3 rounded-lg mb-3 border-l-3 ' + (borderColorMap[section.badgeColor] || '') + '">' + section.note + '</p>';
                }

                html += '<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-xs">';
                html += '<thead class="bg-gray-50"><tr>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-left font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.guest', 'Misafir') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-center font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.checkin', 'Giriş') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-center font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.checkout', 'Çıkış') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-center font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('common.night', 'Gece') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-left font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.platform', 'Platform') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.amount', 'Tutar') + '</th>';
                html += '<th class="py-2 px-3 text-right font-semibold text-gray-500">' + t('proj.col.per.night', '/Gece') + '</th>';
                html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';

                let totalAmount = 0, totalNights = 0;

                section.reservations.forEach(function(res) {
                    const nights = res.nights || Math.max(1, Math.round((new Date(res.checkOut) - new Date(res.checkIn)) / 86400000));
                    const amount = res.totalPrice ?? res.totalAmount ?? 0;
                    const currency = res.currency || 'EUR';
                    const amountEUR = convertEUR(amount, currency);
                    const perNight = nights > 0 ? amountEUR / nights : 0;
                    totalAmount += amountEUR;
                    totalNights += nights;
                    const platformColor = (res.platform === 'Airbnb') ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-600' : (res.platform === 'Booking.com') ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700' : 'bg-green-50 text-green-600';

                    html += '<tr class="border-t border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50">';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 font-medium text-gray-700">' + (res.guestName || '-') + '</td>';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-center text-gray-600">' + fmt(res.checkIn) + '</td>';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-center text-gray-600">' + fmt(res.checkOut) + '</td>';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3 text-center font-semibold">' + nights + '</td>';
                    html += '<td class="py-2 px-3"><span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ' + platformColor + '">' + (res.platform || '-') + '</span></td>';
